*Jonas Schäfer,Cezary Pilaszewicz,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DEW是一种针对大型语言模型的语义水印方案，通过结合上下文和词元级别的嵌入来增强对释义和翻译的鲁棒性，实验表明其在保持文本质量的同时显著提升了检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义水印在面临释义攻击和翻译攻击时鲁棒性不足，容易失效，亟需一种能够优雅退化的水印方案来保障LLM生成文本的可追溯性。

Method: 利用信号处理的方法，对词元嵌入和上下文嵌入进行代数向量空间运算以生成水印信号；通过伪随机矩阵（由密钥种子生成）对嵌入向量进行投影来混淆水印；基于相关代数分布进行统计检验和基准测试。

Result: 在多个LLM上的实验表明，DEW在释义后检测性能提升，保持有竞争力的文本质量；翻译后仍可检测，而此前语义水印显著退化。

Conclusion: DEW是一种实用且鲁棒的LLM文本保护方案，有助于解决负责任的AI部署中的关键问题。

Abstract: This work presents Dual-Embedding Watermarking (DEW), a semantic watermarking scheme for large language models (LLMs) that leverages contextual and token-level embeddings to enhance robustness against paraphrasing and translation. DEW utilizes a signal-processing methodology, applying algebraic vector-space operations to \mbox{token and context embeddings to derive a watermark signal that degrades gracefully under semantic shifts. The method obfuscates the watermark by projecting embedding vectors through pseudo-random matrices seeded with a secret key. Relevant distributions derived from the underlying algebra are evaluated and employed for statistical testing and benchmarking of DEW. Experimental results across multiple LLMs indicate that DEW improves post-paraphrase detection while maintaining competitive text quality, and remains detectable after translation, even when prior semantic watermarks degrade significantly. These findings position DEW as a practical and robust solution for safeguarding LLM-generated text and addressing critical issues in responsible AI deployment.

</details>


### [40] [CLExEval: A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for Qualitative Evaluation of LLM Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31608)
*Ajmal M.,Abin Roy,Afthab Salam Kanniyan,Jawadh Abdul Kabeer,Jerin James,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLExEval是一个基于人机协作的框架，用于在渐进信息遮蔽条件下评估LLM的临床推理能力。通过对40个罕见诊断案例、200条推理轨迹和5600条专家标注的分析，发现了三种反复出现的失败模式：冗长偏差（GPT-4o-mini在信息稀缺下诊断准确率从95.0%降至32.5%）、隐藏知识悖论（专科模型达到92.5%最大诊断潜力但在冗长语境中无法可靠检索）以及68.6%的推理-输出不匹配。LLM-as-a-Judge评估显示GPT-4o-mini批准了47.9%的临床错误输出，表明缺乏专家验证的自动化评估会高估临床可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学基准测试上表现强劲，但其临床推理过程难以可靠评估，存在“评估幻觉”风险：流畅且结构良好的解释即使最终诊断错误也可能在临床上令人信服，因此需要一个更可靠的评估框架。

Method: 提出CLExEval框架，采用人机协作和渐进信息遮蔽策略，结合5600条专家医师标注与200条临床推理轨迹（来自40个罕见诊断案例），系统分析LLM的推理失败模式，并评估LLM-as-a-Judge范式的可靠性。

Result: 发现三个关键失败模式：冗长偏差（信息稀缺时准确率大幅下降）、隐藏知识悖论（模型具备知识但在特定语境下无法检索）、以及68.6%的推理-输出不匹配。LLM-as-a-Judge评估中GPT-4o-mini批准了47.9%的错误输出，HuatuoGPT-o1承认所有有效评分失败并表现出正向自我偏好偏差。

Conclusion: 缺乏专家验证的自动化临床评估可能显著高估LLM的临床可靠性，CLExEval框架为评估LLM临床推理提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong results on many medical benchmarks, but their clinical reasoning remains difficult to evaluate reliably. A central risk is an evaluation illusion: fluent and well-structured explanations can appear clinically convincing even when the final diagnosis is incorrect. We introduce CLExEval, a human-in-the-loop framework for evaluating LLM clinical reasoning under progressive information masking. CLExEval combines 5,600 expert-physician annotations with 200 clinical reasoning traces derived from 40 rare diagnostic cases. Our analysis identifies three recurring failure patterns: (i) verbosity bias, where GPT-4o-mini's diagnostic accuracy drops from 95.0% to 32.5% under information scarcity; (ii) a hidden knowledge paradox, where a specialist model reaches 92.5% maximum diagnostic potential but fails to retrieve that knowledge reliably in verbose contexts; and (iii) a 68.6% reasoning-to-output mismatch, where correct diagnoses appear in reasoning traces but are not reflected in final answers. We further evaluate the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm on a human-verified failure set (n = 142). GPT-4o-mini approved 47.9% of clinically incorrect outputs, while HuatuoGPT-o1 approved all validly scored failures and showed a positive self-preference bias. These results suggest that standalone automated clinical evaluations can substantially overestimate clinical reliability without expert-grounded validation.

</details>

*Kesego Mokgosi,Vukosi Marivate,Sitwala Mundia,Unarine Netshifhefhe,Tsholofelo Hope Mogale,Thapelo Sindane*

TL;DR: 针对6种南部班图语（超8000万使用者），提出基于音调条件的分阶段课程学习框架（混合难度评分+门控适配器+音调统计驱动训练），将零样本WER从100%以上降至28.41%平均WER，发现W2V-BERT对恩古尼语族更优、Whisper对索托-茨瓦纳语族更优，建议按语言配对模型部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础ASR模型对南部班图语零样本WER超过100%，无法用于教育和公共服务等实际场景，亟需针对这些低资源语言的有效语音识别方法。

Method: 提出音调条件分阶段课程学习框架，包含：（1）混合难度评分；（2）基于音调统计的门控适配器；（3）分阶段课程训练。在社区语料库上训练，并在NCHLT上测试迁移鲁棒性。

Result: W2V-BERT在恩古尼语族上比Whisper低3-4% WER，Whisper在索托-茨瓦纳语族上更优；W2V-BERT加音调条件达到28.41%平均WER，Xitsonga迁移上达23.79% WER。

Conclusion: 没有单一模型适用于所有6种语言，实际部署应根据语言配对模型选择，并在多个语料库上验证。

Abstract: Southern Bantu languages are spoken by over 80 million people, yet current foundation ASR models still produce zero-shot WER above 100%, which limits practical use in education and public services. We addressed this gap with a tone conditioned curriculum framework for 6 Southern Bantu languages that combined hybrid difficulty scoring, gated adapters driven by tonal statistics and staged curriculum training. We trained on a community corpus and tested transfer to NCHLT to measure robustness beyond matched evaluation. Results revealed clear interactions between architecture and language, with W2V-BERT outperforming Whisper on Nguni languages by 3 to 4 WER points whilst Whisper performed better on Sotho-Tswana languages. W2V-BERT with tone conditioning reached 28.41% average WER across datasets and 23.79% on Xitsonga transfer. No single model suited all 6 languages, so deployment should pair model selection per language with validation across corpora.

</details>


### [42] [Moral Safety in LLMs: Exposing Performative Compliance with Puzzled Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31644)
*Mohammadamin Shafiei,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型在道德伦理行为上存在"表演性合规"现象：当模型明确感知到自身正在被评估公平性时（如人口统计身份以显式标签呈现），它会表现得更加公平；当同样的身份信息需要模型自行推断时，其公平性显著下降（有害决策增加4.4个百分点），且模型安全排名发生变化。作者提出"线索可见性差距"（Cue Visibility Gap）作为衡量模型真实道德鲁棒性的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在医疗、法律、招聘等具有道德后果的领域承担关键角色，需要检验其伦理行为是真实的还是表面的。现有公平性评估可能高估了模型的安全水平，因为它们以显式标签的形式呈现人口统计身份，这给模型提供了"被评估"的线索，导致模型做出表演性的公平行为。

Method: 提出线索变化（cue-variation）方法论：在保持道德困境和人口统计身份固定的前提下，仅改变身份信息的呈现方式（显式标签 vs. 需推断）。设计了"线索可见性差距"（Cue Visibility Gap）作为模型无关的鲁棒性度量指标，可添加到现有公平性基准中。

Result: 隐藏显式标签导致有害决策增加+4.4个百分点，模型安全排名发生变化。即使在模型正确推断出人口统计身份的情况下，这种不公平行为的偏移仍然存在，排除了属性归因错误的干扰。

Conclusion: 当前缺乏线索变化的公平性评估测得的只是表面合规性而非真正的道德鲁棒性，不应作为高风险场景下部署决策的依据。需要引入线索可见性差距等指标来区分真实的道德安全与表演性合规。

Abstract: As large language models take on morally consequential roles in healthcare, legal, and hiring contexts, we need to examine whether their ethical behaviors are genuine or superficial. We show that current fairness evaluations substantially overestimate moral safety. Models appear fair when demographic identity is stated as an explicit label, yet become measurably less fair when the same identity must be inferred. We term this failure \emph{performative compliance}, where a model is fair when the presentation resembles a fairness evaluation and less fair as that cue weakens. We introduce a cue-variation methodology that holds the moral dilemma and the demographic identity fixed and varies only how that identity is conveyed. Hiding the explicit label raises harmful decisions by $+4.4$~pp and changes model safety rankings, and the shift persists when models correctly infer the demographic, ruling out attribution error. We propose the \textbf{Cue Visibility Gap}, a model-agnostic robustness metric that can be added to any existing fairness benchmark to separate genuine from performative moral safety. Fairness evaluations that omit cue variation measure surface compliance, not moral robustness, and should not ground deployment decisions in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [43] [Overview of the TalentCLEF 2026: Skill and Job Title Intelligence for Human Capital Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31692)
*Luis Gasco,Hermenegildo Fabregat,Laura García-Sardiña,Paula Estrella,Warre Veys,Casimiro Pío Carrino,Matthias De Lange,Daniel Deniz Cerpa,Álvaro Rodrigo,Jens-Joris Decorte,Rabih Zbib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TalentCLEF挑战赛第二版（CLEF 2026实验室），包含两项任务：任务A（上下文相关的人岗匹配）和任务B（岗位-技能匹配与技能类型分类），吸引了113个注册团队和400多次提交，展示了人力资源管理中NLP研究的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动自然语言处理在人力资源管理领域的研究，通过建立共享评估基准来促进人岗匹配和技能匹配技术的发展。

Method: 组织了两项任务：任务A针对职位空缺进行简历候选人的识别与排序（英/西双语），任务B针对职位标题检索相关技能并区分核心技能与上下文技能（英语）。

Result: 吸引了113个注册团队，收到超过400次提交，表明研究社区对人力资源管理共享评估基准的兴趣日益增长。

Conclusion: TalentCLEF挑战赛成功为人力资源管理领域的NLP研究提供了标准化评估平台，促进了相关技术的发展。

Abstract: This paper presents an overview of the second edition of the TalentCLEF challenge, organized as a Lab at the Conference and Labs of the Evaluation Forum (CLEF) 2026. TalentCLEF is an initiative aimed at advancing Natural Language Processing research in Human Capital Management. The second edition of the challenge consisted of two tasks: Task A, contextualized job-person matching, focuses on identifying and ranking the most suitable candidates represented by their resumes for a given job vacancy in English and Spanish. Task B, job-skill matching with skill type classification, addresses retrieving the most relevant skills for a given job title in English and distinguishing between core and contextual skills. TalentCLEF attracted 113 registered teams and received more than 400 submissions in the two tasks, reflecting the growing interest of the research community in shared evaluation benchmarks for Human Capital Management. This paper describes the motivation and organization of the challenge, summarizes the datasets and evaluation settings, and reports the main results obtained by the participating teams.

</details>

*Dragos-Mitrut Vasile,Elena-Simona Apostol,Stefan-Adrian Toma,Adrian Paschke,Ciprian-Octavian Truica*

TL;DR: 本文研究了低资源语言罗马尼亚语的跨语言关系抽取，通过LLM翻译SemEval-2010 Task 8基准数据集，评估Gemma 4 31B在零样本、少样本和QLoRA微调下的表现。结果显示，QLoRA微调可大幅提升F1分数（>22pp）并缩小跨语言差距（降至1.4pp），但125M参数的罗马尼亚语BERT在性能上已接近31B的Gemma，质疑了大模型在单任务RE中的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言关系抽取面临标注语料匮乏的困境，现有方法难以有效扩展到罗马尼亚语等资源稀缺语言，因此探索自动数据集翻译与LLM推理相结合的跨语言RE可行性。

Method: 使用LLM翻译管道将SemEval-2010 Task 8从英语翻译为罗马尼亚语；在Gemma 4 31B上评估零样本、少样本和QLoRA微调配置；与四种编码器基线（XLM-RoBERTa base/large、罗马尼亚语BERT、RoBERT-large）对比；测试两种任务形式：带标记实体的关系分类和端到端抽取。

Result: 罗马尼亚语相比英语在纯提示设置下性能下降3-5pp；少样本相比零样本提升甚微；QLoRA微调使两种语言的宏F1提升超过22pp，跨语言差距从3.3pp降至1.4pp；125M参数的罗马尼亚语BERT在性能上接近31B的Gemma（差距仅1-4pp），且优于278M的多语言XLM-R。

Conclusion: 在计算资源受限的场景下，使用31B大模型进行单一任务关系抽取的性价比不高，小规模单语模型已能取得相近甚至更好的效果。研究发布了翻译数据集、评估代码和训练模型。

Abstract: Relation extraction (RE) for low-resource languages is typically constrained by the lack of annotated corpora. We investigate the feasibility of cross-lingual RE for Romanian by combining automatic dataset translation with large language model (LLM) inference. We translate the SemEval-2010 Task 8 benchmark from English to Romanian using an LLM-based translation pipeline and evaluate Gemma 4 31B under zero-shot, few-shot, and QLoRA fine-tuned configurations, against four encoder baselines spanning 125M to 560M parameters: XLM- RoBERTa (base and large), Romanian BERT, and RoBERT- large. We assess two task formulations: relation classification with marked entities and end-to-end extraction. Our results show that Romanian incurs a 3 to 5 percentage point (pp) drop relative to English in prompt-only settings, that few-shot prompting provides marginal gains over zero-shot, and that QLoRA fine-tuning improves macro F1-Score by more than 22 percentage points in both languages while reducing the cross-lingual gap from 3.3 to 1.4pp. The encoder baselines come within 1-4pp of QLoRA Gemma on Romanian despite being 50-250 times smaller, with monolingual Romanian BERT at 125M parameters matching multilingual XLM-R at 278M. The case for using a 31B model for single-task RE on Romanian is therefore weak in deployment scenarios where compute matters. We release the translated dataset, evaluation code, and trained models.

</details>


### [45] [Seeing Is Not Sharing: Some Vision-Language Models Overestimate Common Ground in Asymmetric Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31719)
*Nan Li,Albert Gatt,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了VLM是否能区分对话参与者之间“可能共享”和“已共享”的信息，发现地图内容（图像或文本）会误导模型过度预测对齐，将潜在共识错误视为已建立共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作对话中，共享感知并不等于共享理解，共同基础（common ground）需要通过交互来建立。作者希望探究VLM是否能区分“可共享”（潜在的）与“已共享”（通过对话确立的）信息。

Method: 在HCRC MapTask对话数据集的13,077个标注指代表达上进行解释匹配任务，系统性地操控对话上下文和地图信息访问方式（真实地图图像、文本描述、无信息图像），评估多个VLM的性能。

Result: 提供真实地图图像提升了整体性能，但导致模型过度预测对齐（over-predicting alignment）；文本描述复现了该偏差；无信息图像则完全抑制了对齐预测。偏差由任务相关的地图内容驱动，而非视觉通道本身。模型依赖于地图上的静态指称线索，而非追踪对话历史中的共同基础建立过程。

Conclusion: 存在偏差的模型将地图内容（无论视觉还是文本形式）视为相互理解的证据，混淆了潜在共同基础与已建立的共同基础。这一现象在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct中最为明显，并在多个模型中有所体现。

Abstract: In collaborative dialogue, shared perception does not guarantee shared interpretation. Mutual understanding must be established through interaction. We investigate whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish what could be shared from what has been shared between dialogue participants through grounding. We formulate this as an interpretation-matching task on 13,077 annotated reference expressions from HCRC MapTask dialogues, and evaluate VLMs under systematically controlled manipulations of dialogue context and map-information access. Our results show that providing authentic map images improves overall performance but shifts models toward over-predicting alignment. Textual descriptions of the same map content reproduce this bias, while non-informative images suppress alignment predictions entirely, indicating that the bias is driven by task-relevant map content, not the visual channel. This improvement comes at the cost of degraded accuracy on non-aligned cases. Calibration analysis and reference-chain tracking further suggest that models rely on static referential cues on the maps rather than tracking how grounding unfolds through dialogue history. We observe these patterns most clearly in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and, to varying degrees, in four additional models from two architecture families. In models that exhibit the bias, map content, whether presented visually or textually, is treated as evidence of mutual understanding, conflating potential with established common ground.

</details>


### [46] [Adapting Foundation ASR Models to Dysarthric Speech: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31722)
*Christian Huber,Laura Kernahan,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对构音障碍语音识别难题，通过微调Whisper基础模型，使用92小时朗读语音和8.8小时用户校正数据，将词错误率降至9.7%，验证了个性化微调在构音障碍ASR中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构音障碍患者的语音与正常语音差异大，导致传统ASR系统性能严重下降，影响了这些患者在日常交流中使用语音识别技术。作者旨在为构音障碍者构建一个实用、高性能的个性化ASR系统。

Method: 使用TEQST工具收集92小时朗读语音数据，并通过部署的移动应用额外收集8.8小时用户校正数据。以Whisper为基础模型进行微调，同时尝试了LoRA适配和Qwen3-ASR作为基础模型进行对比实验。

Result: 仅用1.4小时适配数据微调Whisper即达到15.8%的词错误率；使用22.5小时数据降至10.7%；使用全部数据（含校正数据）达到最佳9.7%的WER。LoRA适配和Qwen3-ASR基础模型效果较差。

Conclusion: 个性化微调能够显著提升基础ASR模型在构音障碍语音上的性能，使其达到实际部署可用的水平。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often perform poorly in dysarthric speech, limiting their usefulness to affected speakers in everyday communication. This paper presents a personalized ASR system for a dysarthric speaker, built by adapting a foundation ASR model to speaker-specific data. Using the TEQST tool, we collected 92 hours of read speech and later added 8.8 hours of user corrections gathered through a deployed mobile application. Starting from Whisper, fine-tuning reduced word error rate to 15.8% with only 1.4 hours of adaptation data, reached 10.7% with 22.5 hours, and achieved the best result of 9.7% when using all available data including the corrections. Using LoRA adaptation and/or Qwen3-ASR as foundation model performed worse in this setting. The results show that personalized fine-tuning can make foundation ASR models substantially more effective for dysarthric speech and suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [47] [STEB: Style Text Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31741)
*Rafael Rivera Soto,Anna Wegmann,Cristina Aggazzotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STEB基准，整合96个数据集、7种语言，用于标准化风格嵌入评估。发现语义嵌入在风格任务中持续失败，且没有通用最优的风格嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义嵌入已有MTEB等标准化基准，但风格嵌入的评估零散、缺乏统一标准，各工作使用各自的任务和数据集，导致无法公平比较不同风格嵌入方法。

Method: 构建STEB基准，包含96个数据集、7种语言，覆盖作者验证、作者检索、AI文本检测、语言特征探针等多种风格相关任务，开源代码库供社区使用。

Result: 语义嵌入在风格任务中表现持续失败；没有任何一种风格嵌入在所有任务上 universally superior。

Conclusion: 风格嵌入研究亟需标准化基准STEB；当前风格嵌入方法各有优劣，需针对具体任务选择；语义嵌入不适合风格任务。

Abstract: While semantic embeddings are rigorously evaluated on the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, the evaluation of style embeddings remains fragmented, with each work relying on their own set of tasks and datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Style Text Embedding Benchmark, a comprehensive open-source benchmark intended to standardize the evaluation of style embeddings. STEB encompasses 96 datasets across 7 languages, spanning applications such as authorship verification, authorship retrieval, AI-text detection, probing of linguistic features, and others. We find that semantic embeddings consistently fail in stylistic tasks, and that there is no style embedding that is universally superior across all tasks evaluated. We open-source the STEB code base at: https://github.com/rrivera1849/STEB.

</details>


### [48] [CHERRY: Compressed Hierarchical Experts with Recurrent Representational Yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31796)
*Dohyeon Kwon,Youngjin Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种提升计算效率的语言模型训练技术：(1)选择性监督(SGT)仅对约15%的语义负载token进行监督，通过梯度耦合使其余85%无监督token也能显著改进，实现4.5倍每监督token效率；(2)深度压缩与循环恢复，将48层1B模型压缩至6层227M，通过循环展开恢复至34有效层，达到2.5倍参数缩减；(3)压缩专家融合(MoEE)结合多token预测，进一步超越单个模型。在CHERRY-1.8B韩语模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索互补性的计算高效语言模型训练技术，在保持模型质量的同时大幅降低计算和参数开销。

Method: (1) Selective Ground Truth Token Training (SGT)：仅对语义负载token（约15%）进行监督训练，利用正梯度耦合系数促进无监督token的改进；(2) 深度压缩+循环恢复：通过相邻层平均将48层压缩至6层，再通过循环展开恢复深度；(3) Mixture of Efficient Experts (MoEE)：融合多个压缩专家模型并结合多token预测。

Result: SGT实现4.5倍每监督token效率，约67%的全序列损失降低来自15%的监督；深度压缩实现2.5倍参数缩减（1B→227M），损失仅从2.926升至2.934；MoEE（2专家）损失2.789优于单模型2.926。

Conclusion: 三种技术可互补组合，显著提升语言模型训练的计算效率，且证明梯度耦合效果依赖于自然语言结构，在乱序文本上消失。论文明确说明了证据范围的局限性（单一模型族、韩语数据、基于损失的评估）。

Abstract: We study three complementary techniques for training compute-efficient language models.
  (1) Selective supervision and per-token efficiency. Selective Ground Truth Token Training (SGT) concentrates supervision on the ~15% of output tokens that carry semantic payload. Through positive gradient coupling in position-shared transformer weights -- a token-level instance of auxiliary-task transfer -- the remaining 85% of unsupervised tokens still improve substantially, giving a 4.5x per-supervised-token efficiency (at the step-100 eval optimum, ~67% of the full-sequence loss reduction is recovered from 15% of the supervision). We prove that this improvement on unsupervised tokens is guaranteed whenever the gradient coupling coefficient gamma-bar = 0.72 is positive (Theorem 1), and show the effect is a property of natural-language structure: it collapses on shuffled text.
  (2) Depth compression with recurrent recovery. A 48-layer, 1B-parameter transformer is compressed to 6 layers (227M) by averaging adjacent layers and restored through learned recurrent unrolling. With 34 effective recurrent layers it reaches a held-out loss of 2.934, within measurement noise of a 566M dense model at 2.926 -- a 2.5x reduction in parameters.
  (3) Fusion of compressed experts. Assembling several compressed models as a Mixture of Efficient Experts (MoEE) with multi-token prediction improves over each single expert at comparable active parameters: a 2-expert MoEE reaches loss 2.789 versus 2.926 for the best single compressed model.
  We validate these techniques on CHERRY-1.8B, a Korean foundation model whose every trainable parameter derives from our own training runs. We are explicit throughout about the scope of the evidence (one model family, Korean data, loss-based metrics) and about which claims are established versus prospective.

</details>


### [49] [Explicit Fuzzy Logic in the Feed-Forward Layer: Self-Forgetting Quantifiers Discover Legible Grammatical-Licensing Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31845)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出参数中性的NC-FFN替换方案，将前馈层每个隐藏单元显式建模为模糊集运算（交集A*B和集合差A*(1-B)），通过引入序列量词（软存在量词和软比例量词）解决逻辑局部化与语法缺陷问题，实现在保持语言模型质量的同时使FFN层可解释——每个单元成为语法许可检测器，无需字典学习即可解读其语义功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的前馈子层虽然具体化了注意力收集的区分信息，但对其计算内容缺乏解释。现有门控/双线性单元缺乏有界正否定（"A但不B"）能力，而该能力对逻辑推理至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出NC-FFN：每个隐藏单元为模糊集运算（交集A*B和集合差A*(1-B)）；2) 引入序列量词模块：软存在量词和软比例量词，每个单元带可学习的遗忘率（从粘性初始化开始）；3) 在N-bit奇偶校验任务和125M参数OpenWebText语言模型上进行验证。

Result: NC-FFN在N-bit奇偶校验上以最浅深度实现最高参数效率；在125M规模上GELU基线困惑度持平；序列量词在第一个epoch即恢复语法缺陷（将更宽的epoch-2差距减半），在LAMBADA上略有领先；语义层单元无需字典学习即可读作语法许可检测器（比较级、被动分词、负极词等）。

Conclusion: 实现了参数中性、语言模型质量的Transformer，其前馈层具有可读的、通过构造可解释的语法机制——不仅说明了FFN层表示什么，还解释了它如何进行语法许可。完全布尔化的FFN在训练中发散，表明可解释性仅在一定分区内自由实现。

Abstract: A transformer's feed-forward (FFN) sublayer materializes the distinctions attention gathers, yet gives no account of what it computes. In a parameter-neutral replacement, each hidden unit is an explicit fuzzy set operation on sigmoid-bounded [0,1] memberships: intersection A*B and set-difference A*(1-B), the latter a bounded positive negation ("A but not B") that gated/bilinear units lack -- a negation-capable FFN (NC-FFN). On N-bit parity they are the most parameter-efficient reasoning basis at shallow depth; at scale (125M, OpenWebText) NC-FFN ties the GELU baseline's perplexity, every unit carrying explicit logical form. Two limits share one cause: two-operand logic localizes to layer 0 and erodes under training, and the one robust grammatical deficit concentrates in licensing and quantifiers, beyond within-token operators. We resolve both with a small block of sequence quantifiers: a soft existential and a soft proportion, each with a per-unit learned forgetting rate from a sticky init. This recovers the deficit at epoch one (halving the wider epoch-two gap), modestly leads on LAMBADA, and makes the FFN legible: the structure now holds and migrates into depth; the decay un-learns its stickiness (median half-life ~1.5 tokens; zero latch units); and at the semantic layers the units read, without dictionary learning, as grammatical licensing detectors: each fires on a licensor (a comparative, a passive participle, a negative-polarity item) and carries its memory forward to predict the licensed word (than, by, nor). This legibility is localized and free only up to a partition (a fully Boolean FFN diverges in training), but the result is a parameter-neutral, language-model-quality transformer with a readable, interpretable-by-construction grammatical mechanism -- an account not just of what a feed-forward layer represents but how it licenses.

</details>


### [50] [Theory of Mind and Persuasion Beyond Conversation: Assessing the Capacity of LLMs to Induce Belief States via Planning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31916)
*Ben Slater,Matteo G. Mecattaf,Lucy G. Cheke,John Burden,Winnie Street*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出非对话式规划心理理论（NCP-ToM）评估框架NCP-ExploreToM，测试LLM智能体通过行动而非对话诱导他人信念状态的能力。在600个任务实例中对6个前沿模型和人类参与者进行评估，发现GPT-5在约80%任务中成功且唯一超越人类，但所有模型在诱导真实信念任务上表现优于虚假信念任务，为对齐研究提供积极信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM基准多采用被动问答形式，无法评估LLM在日益自主化的智能体场景中通过行动诱导信念状态的能力。这种非对话式规划ToM能力对用户助手交互、教学等场景至关重要，但也存在操纵或 misinformation 风险，亟需新的评估方法。

Method: 提出NCP-ExploreToM框架，颠覆传统任务结构：为模型提供一组信念状态目标，要求其通过移动物体或将角色引导至房间来实现目标，而非通过对话说服。在600个任务实例上评估GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude 4系列等6个前沿模型及人类参与者。

Result: GPT-5在智能体设置中约80%任务成功，是唯一在任务上超越人类参与者的模型，但在不同情境下稳健性不如人类。所有模型在诱导真实信念状态任务上表现优于诱导虚假信念状态任务。

Conclusion: LLM在非对话式任务完成中展现出新兴的社会推理能力，但相较于人类仍不够稳健。所有模型在真实信念任务上表现更好是对齐努力的积极信号。研究强调了智能体评估对理解自主社会智能体的安全与对齐的必要性。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs) typically rely on passive question-answering formats, but the deployment of LLMs in increasingly agentic and autonomous forms demands new evaluations. In this paper we evaluate an agent's ability to induce specific belief states in other agents by taking actions rather than using conversational persuasion, a capability we call Non-Conversational Planning ToM (NCP-ToM). NCP-ToM is likely to be essential for many agent use-cases, including within user-assistant interactions and pedagogical contexts, but may also present manipulation or misinformation risks. Using a novel framework, NCP-ExploreToM, we subvert the conventional task structure by providing models with a set of belief state goals and requiring them to move objects or direct characters into rooms to achieve their goals. We evaluated six frontier models, including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro and the Claude 4 series, and a cohort of human participants, across 600 task instances. GPT-5 was successful on approximately 80% of tasks in the agentic setting, and was the only model to outperform human participants on our task, but was still less robust than humans across contexts. We additionally found that all models, like humans, performed better on tasks inducing true belief states than false belief states, which is a positive signal for alignment efforts. These findings highlight emerging social-reasoning capabilities in LLMs for non-conversational task completion and underscore the necessity of agentic evaluations for understanding the safety and alignment of autonomous social agents.

</details>


### [51] [LuxEmo: Expressive Text-to-Speech Corpus for Luxembourgish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31947)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Sandipana Dowerah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LuxEmo，一个21小时的卢森堡语情感语音数据集，并设计了半自动数据 curation 流程和多种 expressive TTS 系统的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音数据集主要覆盖广泛使用的语言，低资源语言（如卢森堡语）在语音技术研究中严重不足。

Method: 采用半自动数据 curatory 流程，包括VAD、去噪、语言识别、LuxASR分段、自动情感预测、词汇线索和人工审核；并构建了4种情感类别、21小时的数据集。

Result: 成功构建了LuxEmo语料库，并评估了5种 expressive TTS 系统，包括基于德语跨语言迁移、多语言卢森堡语支持、卢森堡语适应和非参数韵律迁移等方法。

Conclusion: LuxEmo为低资源语言卢森堡语的情感语音研究提供了重要资源，验证了半自动 curation 流程的有效性，并建立了 expressive TTS 的基准。

Abstract: State-of-the-art speech datasets predominantly focus on widely spoken languages, often overlooking low-resource languages such as Luxembourgish, which remain underrepresented in speech technology research. In this work, we introduce LuxEmo, a 21-hour conversational expressive speech corpus for Luxembourgish with 4 emotion categories. LuxEmo is derived from Radio Télévision Luxembourg (RTL) youth broadcasts, using automated detection followed by human validation. We propose a semi-automatic curation workflow combining voice activity detection, denoising, language identification, LuxASR-based segmentation, automatic emotion prediction, lexical cues, and targeted human review. Additionally, we benchmark five expressive TTS systems covering German-based cross-lingual transfer, multilingual Luxembourgish support, Luxembourgish adaptation, and non-parametric prosody transfer. Performance is evaluated using both objective metrics and human evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [DigitalCoach: Communication and Grounding Gaps in Human and Agentic Computer Use Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31980)
*Meng Chen,Anya Ji,Tsung-Han Wu,Tobias Maringgele,David M. Chan,Alane Suhr,Amy Pavel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DigitalCoach是一个包含72节人机教学课程、22,752轮对话和28.1小时屏幕记录的多模态数据集。当前模型在教学中更倾向于直接指令，缺乏解释、错误诊断和知识检查，导致学习者被动跟随而非深入参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体能够自动化软件任务，但缺乏教导人类如何使用软件的能力。研究者希望探索AI能否像人类专家一样教会新手使用软件，并为此构建基准数据集。

Method: 收集72节人类专家-新手教学课程的多模态数据（屏幕录制+输入事件+对话），建立DigitalCoach数据集。通过自动化评估（对比人类与模型的教学行为差异）和交互式评估（让真实用户与模型教练互动）两个维度分析模型教学表现。

Result: 自动化评估显示模型提供更多直接指令，但更少解释、错误诊断和知识检查问题。即使固定教学方法，模型生成的语句虽接近人类参考文本，却缺乏视觉情境关联。交互评估表明模型教练导致学习者被动跟随指令，缺乏深度参与，且在视觉接地方面表现不足。

Conclusion: DigitalCoach为构建协作性和主动性的计算机使用教学智能体奠定了基础。当前模型在教学对话中仍与人类存在显著差距，尤其在解释性、诊断性和情境感知能力上。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly capable of automating software tasks, but can they teach humans how to use software themselves? We introduce DigitalCoach, a multimodal dataset of 72 human expert-novice computer use coaching sessions consisting of 22,752 dialogue turns grounded in 28.1 hours of screen and input event recordings across five software applications. We use DigitalCoach to evaluate whether state-of-the-art models can teach humans how to use computers. Automated evaluation shows that models differ from humans in how they coach: models provide more direct instructions, but fewer explanations, error diagnoses, and knowledge-check questions. When we fix the coaching method, models produce utterances similar to human references yet poorly grounded in visual context. Interactive evaluation confirms that model coaches cause learners to passively follow instructions without deeper engagement and fall short in visual grounding. DigitalCoach lays a foundation for collaborative and proactive computer use coaching agents.

</details>


### [53] [Scalable Behaviour Cloning on Browser Using via Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32014)
*Kaisen Yang,Zheng Jiang,Yuzhao Peng,Houde Qian,Boshi Zhang,Youjie Zheng,Shijin Hong,Qingle Liu,Ruoyu Han,Bohan Lyu,Bingxiang He,Eren Cai,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过技能蒸馏（skill distillation）从人类浏览器交互轨迹中提取可复用的自然语言技能，并组织成技能图（skill graph），从而解决浏览器代理在决策时信息不完整的问题，实现可扩展的行为克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网用户通过浏览器执行大量技能性工作（如开发、文档编辑、搜索、填表等），这些人类浏览行为是可扩展但未被充分利用的可复用浏览器技能来源。现有浏览器代理的瓶颈在于信息不完整下的决策，而非底层操作，而代理缺乏的先验知识已隐含在人类交互轨迹中。

Method: 采用可扩展的行为克隆方法，通过技能蒸馏将用户交互轨迹转换为紧凑的自然语言技能，代理可直接读取、检索、复用和组合这些技能。进一步将这些蒸馏得到的技能组织成技能图，使得技能增长通过整合（consolidation）而非无界累积来实现。

Result: 提出浏览器代理的可扩展性可能更多来自互联网用户已在表达的集体技能，而非手工设计的任务。项目已开源在 https://lab.einsia.ai/browserbc/。

Conclusion: 浏览器代理的瓶颈在于信息不完整下的决策，通过从人类交互轨迹中蒸馏自然语言技能并组织成技能图，可实现可扩展的行为克隆，为浏览器代理的技能获取提供了一条基于人类集体智慧的新路径。

Abstract: Internet users collectively perform an enormous range of skilled work through web browsers, from software development and document editing to search, forms, and enterprise workflows, making human browsing a highly scalable but under-exploited source of reusable browser skills. We argue that the bottleneck for browser agents is decision-making under incomplete information rather than low-level operation, and that the priors agents lack are already implicit in human interaction traces. We therefore study scalable behavior cloning for browser agents via skill distillation, converting user interaction trajectories into compact natural-language skills that agents can read, retrieve, reuse, and compose directly. We further organize the distilled skills into a skill graph so that growth proceeds through consolidation rather than unbounded accumulation. This suggests that the scalability of browser agents may come less from manually designed tasks and more from the collective skills already expressed by internet users. Our project is available at: https://lab.einsia.ai/browserbc/.

</details>


### [54] [Generative Skill Composition for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32025)
*Xinyu Zhao,Zhen Tan,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Nakul Agarwal,Kwonjoon Lee,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillComposer，一种结构化技能组合方法，通过约束自回归解码器联合预测技能子集、数量和顺序，在两个生产级编码Agent上将通过率提升18-23个百分点，达到与金牌技能检索上界相当的性能且提示词成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent的技能选择和组合方法（全量推理或向量检索）忽略了技能组合的结构化本质——子集、数量和顺序三个维度是耦合的，不能独立决策。

Method: SkillComposer将结构化技能组合形式化为任务条件下的技能序列预测任务，使用约束自回归解码器在技能标识符上逐位生成，使子集、数量和顺序在一次解码过程中联合涌现，并自然捕获技能间的依赖关系。

Result: 在GPT-5.2-Codex和Gemini-3-Pro-Preview上，SkillComposer相比无技能基线分别提升+23.1和+18.2个百分点，超越Top-3检索方法，达到与金牌技能检索上界相当的性能，同时提示词成本更低。

Conclusion: 结构化技能组合是一种有效的范式，通过联合预测技能子集、数量和顺序，能够以更低成本实现最优的技能检索效果，为Agent技能库的高效复用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent LLM agents benefit from skills for solving complex tasks. Skills encapsulate modular packages of procedural knowledge and instructions for performing specialized tasks, such as setting up a sandboxed environment, running a test suite, or refactoring a function across multiple files. As skill libraries grow and become reusable across tasks and domains, selecting an appropriate skill composition has emerged as a central bottleneck. Existing approaches fall into two categories. One exposes the agent's reasoning to the entire skill collection; the other performs skill retrieval via embeddings or LLM-based rerankers. Both provide useful insights; however, they miss the structural nature of skill composition, which is a joint decision over which skills, how many, and in what order -- three dimensions that cannot be decoupled. We formalize this as structured skill composition: given a task and a skill library, predict an executable skill plan that jointly specifies the activated subset, count, and execution order. We propose SkillComposer, which instantiates structured skill composition as task-conditioned skill sequence prediction. SkillComposer uses a constrained autoregressive decoder over skill identifiers, so subset, count, and order emerge jointly from a single decoding pass, and dependencies between successive skills are captured naturally. We build a training set of task-composition pairs from a real, human-curated skill library. We then evaluate SkillComposer along two axes: composition quality on a held-out test set, and downstream task success on SkillsBench across two production-grade coding agents. On GPT-5.2-Codex, Gemini-3-Pro-Preview, SkillComposer raises the pass rate by +23.1, +18.2pp over the no-skill baseline, surpassing top-3 retrieval and matching the gold-skill retrieval upper bound at lower prompt-token cost.

</details>


### [55] [When LLMs Read Tables Carelessly: Measuring and Reducing Data Referencing Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32029)
*Yuqing Yang,Qi Zhu,Zhen Han,Boran Han,Zhengyuan Shen,Shuai Wang,Vassilis N. Ioannidis,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地评估了大型语言模型在表格任务中的数据引用错误（DREs），发现所有测试模型（1.7B-20B参数）均存在该问题，并提出基于批评模型的数据引用检测方法，通过批评模型过滤和拒绝采样将答案准确率提升最高12.0%，训练了一个4B参数的轻量级批评模型，在分布内和分布外DRE检测中平均F1达78.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在处理表格任务时虽然能理解表结构，但仍会犯数据引用错误（DREs），即错误引用或遗漏表格数值。这类错误不仅影响最终答案准确性，更直接损害中间推理步骤的正确性和可靠性。然而，以往研究仅提供了有限的小规模分析，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 1) 对不同模型（1.7B至20B参数）在不同表格任务上进行系统性DRE评估；2) 提出将数据引用作为批评模型（critic）的方法，通过批评模型过滤和拒绝采样来提升答案准确率；3) 训练一个4B参数的轻量级批评模型用于检测DRE。

Result: 1) 所有测试模型均存在DRE问题；2) 基于批评模型的过滤和拒绝采样方法可将答案准确率提升最高12.0%；3) 训练的4B批评模型在分布内和分布外DRE检测中平均F1得分为78.2%，并能有效辅助更大模型的推理。

Conclusion: 数据引用错误是LLM在表格任务中的普遍问题，通过引入专门的批评模型进行检测和过滤，可以有效提升模型答案的准确性和可靠性，轻量级批评模型也能有效辅助大型模型。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) perform well on table tasks, they still make data referencing errors (DREs), i.e., incorrectly citing or omitting table values, despite understanding the table structure. Beyond final-answer accuracy, DREs directly compromise the correctness and reliability of intermediate reasoning steps. Yet prior studies have only offered limited, small-scale analyses. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of tabular data referencing errors across different models and tasks. Our results show that DREs occur across all tested models (1.7B to 20B parameters). Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating data referencing as a critic significantly improves answer accuracy up to 12.0%, through critic-based filtering and rejection sampling. Finally, we trained a lightweight 4B-parameter critic model that achieves an average F1 score of 78.2% in detecting both in-distribution and out-of-distribution DREs, and effectively assists inference for larger models.

</details>


### [56] [Reinforcement Learning with Metacognitive Feedback Elicits Faithful Uncertainty Expression in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32032)
*Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Avi Caciularu,Gal Yona,Idan Szpektor,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于元认知的机制——带元认知反馈的强化学习（RLMF）和元认知数据选择，通过让模型自我判断自身表现来改进其校准能力和元认知能力，实验表明RLMF在忠实校准任务上达到SOTA，性能超越标准强化学习最高达63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在元认知能力上存在系统性缺陷：高置信度幻觉、无法识别知识边界、错误表达内在不确定性，这破坏了其可信度和可靠性。由于监控和调节自身认知过程是智能的关键组成部分，提升模型的元认知能力对于增强其整体能力和对齐至关重要。

Method: 提出两种机制：1) 带元认知反馈的强化学习（RLMF）：在偏好优化过程中，基于模型自身对表现质量的自我判断来细化完成排序；2) 元认知数据选择：利用类似的自我判断来识别高价值训练样本。采用两阶段解耦方法，先校准模型自信度分数的忠实性，再通过目标输出编辑映射到自然语言的不确定性表达。

Result: RLMF在多种任务上实现了可泛化的、SOTA的忠实校准效果，同时保持准确性；比标准强化学习提升高达63%，显著增强了模型评估和表达自身能力边界的能力。

Conclusion: RLMF是一种有前景的增强LLM元认知能力的范式，可同时提升模型能力和对齐水平；元认知表现可作为有效的强化学习信号，克服以往内在反馈方法的局限性。

Abstract: Metacognition is a critical component of intelligence that describes the ability to monitor and regulate one's own cognitive processes. Yet LLMs exhibit systemic deficiencies in key metacognitive faculties: they hallucinate with high confidence, fail to recognize knowledge boundaries, and misrepresent their internal uncertainty--undermining trustworthiness and reliability. Since monitoring task performance and adapting behavior accordingly are central to metacognition, we posit that models capable of accurately judging their own performance are better positioned to improve it. We operationalize this idea via two novel mechanisms: reinforcement learning with metacognitive feedback (RLMF), a paradigm to refine completion rankings during preference optimization based on the quality of a model's self-judgments of performance, and metacognitive data selection, which uses similar self-judgments to identify high-value training examples, outperforming naive active learning. We apply these innovations to the problem of faithful calibration (FC), a task that is itself fundamentally metacognitive: the goal is to align expressed with intrinsic uncertainty, difficult even for frontier LLMs. We adopt a two-stage, decoupled approach, first using these methods to calibrate the faithfulness of models' self-reported confidence scores, then mapping to natural, context-adaptable linguistic uncertainty via targeted output editing. Extensive experiments show RLMF achieves generalizable, state-of-the-art FC on diverse tasks while preserving accuracy. Further, RLMF surpasses standard RL by up to 63% while enhancing models' ability to assess and express their own capability limits. This positions RLMF as a promising paradigm to enhance LLM metacognition toward improved abilities and alignment, and suggests metacognitive performance as an effective RL signal to overcome limits of prior intrinsic feedback methods.

</details>


### [57] [Introspective Coupling: Self-Explanation Training Tracks Behavioral Change Despite Fixed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32038)
*Zifan Carl Guo,Laura Ruis,Jacob Andreas,Belinda Z. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，训练语言模型生成解释（explanation）时，即使使用固定的、来自早期版本或其他模型的反事实解释数据集，模型也能产生比训练目标更忠实于自身行为的"内省性"解释。这种现象发生在训练过程中解释与当前行为保持足够相关时，并且能够追踪行为变化，无需更新监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在训练生成解释时，其解释是否真正反映自身推理过程（忠实内省），还是仅仅模仿训练数据中的表面模式。

Method: 使用来自模型自身早期检查点或行为相似的不同族模型的反事实解释作为固定训练数据，训练LM生成关于输入特征如何影响其行为的解释，然后评估解释与模型当前行为（通过对修改输入的干预行为）之间的一致性。

Result: 模型产生的解释往往比训练目标更忠实于自身当前行为；这种内省耦合发生在解释与行为在训练过程中保持足够相关时；解释训练与其他后训练目标并行时，解释能自动追踪行为变化；该现象在多任务（如谄媚、拒绝）中稳健存在。

Conclusion: 即使使用固定的反事实解释数据集，也能为语言模型提供可扩展、可泛化的内省性后训练信号，实现解释与行为的忠实对齐。

Abstract: When does training language models (LMs) to generate explanations of their predictions yield faithful introspection, rather than superficial imitation? We study LMs trained to explain which features of their inputs influenced their behavior, using models' counterfactual behavior on modified inputs as supervision. Surprisingly, we find that LMs trained on fixed counterfactual explanations derived from earlier checkpoints of themselves, or even from behaviorally similar models in different families, frequently produce explanations more faithful to their own current behaviors than to those of their training targets. This "introspective" coupling between LM explanations and behaviors occurs when training explanations remain sufficiently correlated with current behaviors over the course of training, even as behaviors themselves shift. We also show that introspective coupling tracks behavior shifts: when explanation training is provided concurrently with other post-training objectives, explanations track those shifts without requiring updated supervision. This phenomenon appears in multiple tasks, including sycophancy and refusal, and is robust to label noise. Overall, our results show that even fixed datasets of counterfactual explanations can provide scalable and generalizable post-training signal for introspection.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [58] [Joint discovery of governing partial differential equations from multi-source datasets by competitive optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30699)
*Hao Xu,Siyu Lou,Yuntian Chen,Dongxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCO-PDE框架，通过软竞争加权机制融合多源数据集，结合遗传算法从观测数据中发现共享的偏微分方程，在少量数据下即可高精度恢复经典方程，并适用于复杂几何域和真实实验数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法通常基于单数据集操作，在观测受限时性能有限；而实践中同一物理系统常存在多个仅初始/边界条件不同的数据集，如何有效融合这些异构数据源以发现共享控制方程是一个关键问题。

Method: 首先为每个数据源独立训练神经代理模型，然后通过软竞争加权机制动态评估各数据集的可靠性并聚合共识全局系数，最后结合遗传算法进行结构搜索，同时识别控制方程的函数形式和参数。

Result: 在七个案例中，每个数据集仅使用50个观测数据即可高精度恢复经典方程；该方法能处理二维和三维不规则边界及异构系数区域，并能从真实波浪水槽实验中提取物理上有意义的定律。

Conclusion: 该工作通过异构数据融合为自动科学发现提供了一条有前景的路径，表明多源数据协同能够弥补单数据集观测不足的局限。

Abstract: Discovering governing equations directly from observational data is a key step towards interpretable scientific machine learning. Current data-driven approaches typically operate on a single dataset, inherently limiting their performance when faced with restricted observations. In practice, multiple datasets are often available for the same physical system, distinguished only by distinct initial conditions or boundary configurations. Here, we present a competitive optimization framework designed to discover shared partial differential equations (PDEs) from multi-source datasets, termed MCO-PDE. The framework first trains independent neural surrogates for each data source, and then employs a soft-competitive weighting mechanism to dynamically assess dataset credibility and aggregate a consensus global coefficient. Integrated with a genetic algorithm for structural search, this approach simultaneously identifies the functional forms and parameters of the governing laws. We demonstrate that fusing as few as 50 observations per dataset across seven cases recovers canonical equations with high accuracy. The framework inherently handles two- and three-dimensional domains characterized by irregular boundaries and heterogeneous coefficients, and successfully extracts physically meaningful laws from real-world wave-tank experiments. Overall, this work establishes a promising route for automated scientific discovery via heterogeneous data fusion.

</details>


### [59] [Accelerometry-Derived Digital Biomarkers for Cardiometabolic Risk: A Population-Representative Tabular Benchmark with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30702)
*Federico Felizzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于NHANES 2003-2006数据的加速度计心肺代谢基准，评估了三种表格学习方法（岭回归、XGBoost、TabPFN v2）预测糖化血红蛋白、空腹甘油三酯和C反应蛋白的性能，并采用分裂共形预测评估了人口统计子群覆盖公平性，发现TabPFN v2整体最优，但甘油三酯预测困难，且存在亚组覆盖不均问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化表格数据基准未能反映临床真实世界的特性，如复杂调查抽样、人口统计过采样和子群公平性，因此需要构建更贴近临床实际的新基准。

Method: 使用NHANES 2003-2006数据，包含1381名成人的髋部加速度计数据、空腹实验室生物标志物、膳食摄入和人体测量指标；评估岭回归、XGBoost和TabPFN v2三种方法；应用分裂共形预测生成无分布假设的90%预测区间，并评估性别和种族/民族亚组的覆盖公平性。

Result: TabPFN v2在HbA1c (R²=0.156) 和CRP (R²=0.383) 预测上表现最佳；甘油三酯预测效果差（R²<0.05）；边际覆盖率对CRP和HbA1c接近90%目标，但甘油三酯低于目标；亚组层面存在局部覆盖不足（如墨西哥裔美国人的HbA1c）。

Conclusion: 现有模型在亚组公平性上存在不足，边际保证与临床公平所需的条件覆盖之间存在差距，甘油三酯的预测受遗传因素主导难以通过活动表型改善。

Abstract: Structured tabular data dominates clinical medicine, yet existing benchmarks fail to reflect real-world properties like complex survey sampling, demographic oversampling, and subgroup fairness. We introduce the NHANES Accelerometry Cardiometabolic Benchmark, derived from NHANES 2003-2006, comprising 1,381 adults with hip-worn accelerometry, fasting laboratory biomarkers, dietary intake, and anthropometrics. We evaluate three tabular learning methods -- ridge regression, XGBoost, and the foundation model TabPFN v2 -- to predict glycated haemoglobin (HbA1c), fasting triglycerides, and C-reactive protein (CRP) from activity phenotypes and lifestyle covariates. TabPFN v2 achieves the best overall performance (HbA1c R^2=0.156, CRP R^2=0.383), while triglycerides remain largely unpredictable (R^2 < 0.05), consistent with known genetic dominance. We apply split conformal prediction to generate distribution-free 90% prediction intervals and evaluate demographic coverage equity across sex and race/ethnicity subgroups. Marginal coverage aligns with the 90% target for CRP and HbA1c but falls below for triglycerides. At the subgroup level, we observe localized undercoverage (e.g., HbA1c for Mexican American participants), illustrating the gap between marginal guarantees and the conditional coverage required for clinical fairness. Code and data are at https://github.com/felizzi/nhanes-accel-cardiometabolic-benchmark.

</details>


### [60] [From Search to Synthesis: Training LLMs as Zero-Shot Workflow Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30704)
*Gan Luo,Zihan Qin,Bin Dong,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaFlow将工作流生成建模为元学习问题，通过两阶段训练（监督微调+强化学习）生成可复用的任务级解决方案策略，在问答、代码生成和数学推理任务上达到SOTA水平，并展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的实例级解决方案缺乏结构一致性，而手动设计任务级工作流需要大量专业知识和努力，限制了其广泛应用。现有自动工作流生成方法要么只生实例级方案无法学习任务级模式，要么无法泛化到训练配置之外。

Method: 将工作流生成建模为元学习问题，给定任务和算子集，模型学习组合解决方案策略。两阶段训练：第一阶段在合成工作流数据上进行监督微调；第二阶段使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），利用任务内多个问题实例的执行反馈来改进端到端成功率。

Result: 在问答、代码生成和数学推理基准测试中，MetaFlow在域内任务上通过单次推理达到与SOTA基线相当的性能，同时在域外任务和算子集上展现出显著的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaFlow能够为已训练任务生成高效的工作流，并对未训练任务和新的算子集表现出强泛化能力，有效解决了自动工作流生成中结构一致性与可泛化性的关键挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel across a wide range of tasks, yet their instance-specific solutions often lack the structural consistency needed for reliable deployment. Workflows that encode recurring algorithmic patterns at the task level provide a principled framework, offering robustness across instance variations, interpretable traces for debugging, and reusability across problem instances. However, manually designing such workflows requires significant expertise and effort, limiting their broader application. While automatic workflow generation could address this bottleneck, existing methods either produce instance-specific solutions without learning task-level patterns, or cannot generalize beyond their training configurations. We present MetaFlow, which casts workflow generation as a meta-learning problem: given a task and an operator set, the model learns to compose solution strategies. MetaFlow trains in two stages: supervised fine-tuning on synthetic workflow data, followed by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) that uses execution feedback across problem instances in the task to improve end-to-end success. The resulting model produces effective workflows for trained tasks and exhibits strong generalization to untrained tasks and novel operator sets. Across benchmarks in question answering, code generation, and mathematical reasoning, MetaFlow achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art baselines on in-domain tasks with single inference, while demonstrating remarkable zero-shot generalization capabilities on out-of-domain tasks and operator sets.

</details>


### [61] [Why Do Few-Step Text Latents Fail When Image Latents Work? Non-Commitment at Sharp Categorical Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30705)
*Zhongyao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 确定性连续文本潜变量上的少步生成会崩溃为不连贯文本，原因是几何因素而非训练不足：平滑确定性映射无法在尖锐分类读出前解决离散分支选择。论文证明了后验均值终末步的翻转率由读出尖锐度决定，提出DABI和CCI诊断指标，发现连续文本解码器的DABI高达10⁵而图像解码器≈1，并给出了两种逃逸机制（自回归与随机重注入）及维度相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性少步生成方法在连续图像潜变量上成功，但在连续文本潜变量上失败，表现为崩溃为不连贯文本。作者试图探究这一失败的根源是几何/拓扑层面的固有困难，而非训练或规模不足。

Method: 理论分析：证明后验均值终末步在决策边界附近的翻转率（定理3）；提出DABI（读出尖锐度）和CCI（分类承诺度）诊断指标，在四个独立构建的连续文本解码器上测量；证明确定性少步生成中离散模态分离所需刚度与维度、深度之间的标度律（定理5-7）及余面积恒等式（定理17）。

Result: 连续文本解码器DABI在5×10²至>10⁵之间，图像解码器DABI≈1；确定性ODE在K=4时PPL=294，随机SDE为50；分离模态所需刚度随维度呈Θ(√log M)增长（当维度Ω(log M)时），在固定维度中为M^{1/n}，深度层次结构使每步峰值减少√B。

Conclusion: 确定性连续少步生成的失败源于几何刚性：平滑映射无法在尖锐分类读出前解决离散分支。该不准确性在确定性-连续类中不可约，逃逸需要跳出该类别（自回归提供分类承诺，随机重注入打破平滑约束）。

Abstract: Deterministic few-step generation succeeds on continuous image latents but collapses to incoherent text on continuous text latents, and we show the cause is geometric rather than a training or scaling deficiency: a smooth, regularity-limited deterministic map cannot resolve a discrete branch choice before a sharp categorical readout, so few-step failure is governed by decoder sharpness, not transport accuracy. In the overlapping regime of real text autoencoders, we prove (Theorem 3) that the posterior-mean terminal step flips tokens at the rate of the latent mass in an $O(s(t))$ tube around decision boundaries. Two diagnostics, DABI (readout sharpness) and CCI (categorical commitment), measured on published checkpoints show that four independently built continuous-text decoders amplify a boundary-aligned perturbation far beyond a norm-matched isotropic one (DABI from $5\times10^{2}$ to $>10^{5}$), while image decoders have DABI $\approx 1$. Two mechanisms escape the continuous bound: categorical commitment (autoregressive decoders succeed despite sharper readouts) and stochastic re-injection (deterministic ODE at $K=4$ gives PPL 294 versus SDE 50 on the same model). In the idealized separated regime we prove matching sharp transport laws, including a dimension phase diagram: the deterministic stiffness needed to separate $M$ modes grows as $Θ(\sqrt{\log M})$ once the latent dimension is $Ω(\log M)$ (and as $M^{1/n}$ in fixed dimension), with a depth-$B$ hierarchy giving a $\sqrt{B}$-smaller per-step peak (Theorems 5-7); a coarea identity links these to the overlapping tube (Theorem 17). The result is an accuracy-depth-stiffness tradeoff: within the deterministic-continuous class the cost is irreducible, and both escapes step outside it.

</details>


### [62] [Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30709)
*Woernle Frank,Fedosov Vladimir,Grinenko Artemiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) 是一种即插即用的稀疏注意力方法，替代密集因果注意力，无需重新训练或修改预训练权重。通过两级分层检索（先块级再组级）实现对长上下文的高效注意力计算，在单张RTX 5090（32GB）上支持64K token上下文，每token从RAM/NVMe获取仅约3%的KV数据，困惑度损失仅0.01-0.02 nats。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长上下文Transformer中密集因果注意力随序列长度线性增长的GPU显存瓶颈，使在消费级硬件上运行超长上下文（如64K token）成为可能，同时避免重新训练或额外校准开销。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA)，一种两级分层路由机制：第一级使用紧凑的RoPE感知摘要检索相关块（chunk），第二级在块内进一步筛选相关组（group），仅将选中的token KV从主机RAM/NVMe传输到GPU显存进行精确注意力计算。预训练的W_Q/W_K/W_V/W_O参数完全保留，无需重训练或校准。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，单RTX 5090（32GB）支持64K token上下文；稀疏度仅约3%；路由注意力与密集注意力的KL散度仅为0.01-0.02 nats；GPU显存消耗仅取决于模型权重和路由工作集而非总上下文长度。

Conclusion: 分层路由引入的近似误差很小，剩余质量差距主要由长上下文位置编码主导而非路由算法本身。HGA使得在有限显存的硬件上运行超长上下文成为实用可行方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) is a drop-in replacement for dense causal attention in pretrained long-context transformers. HGA preserves the original checkpoint parameters: the pretrained $W_Q$, $W_K$, $W_V$, and $W_O$ projections remain unchanged, no calibration parameters are introduced, and no retraining is required.
  Applied to Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8 on a single RTX~5090 (32GB), the patched model runs out of the box at a 64K-token context, where token-level K/V storage is not feasible on this hardware.
  Unlike previous sparse-attention methods, HGA performs hierarchical two-level routing. It first retrieves relevant chunks using compact RoPE-aware summaries and then refines the selection by routing only the most relevant groups before performing exact token-level attention. This hierarchical retrieval significantly reduces the number of fetched tokens while preserving exact attention over the retrieved token set, making RAM- and NVMe-backed storage practical.
  The full historical token K/V resides in host RAM or NVMe storage, while only a small routed working set is transferred to GPU memory during attention. Consequently, GPU memory consumption depends primarily on model weights and the routed working set rather than on the total context length.
  Across all tested context lengths (4K - 64K tokens), routed attention remains within approximately $0.01$--$0.02$ nats of dense attention while the sparsity used is just about 3%. These results suggest that the approximation introduced by hierarchical routing is small, and that the remaining quality gap is likely dominated by long-context positional encoding rather than by the routing algorithm itself.

</details>


### [63] [ReactionAtlas: Ab origine exploration of chemical reaction networks with machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30778)
*Stefan Gugler,Max Eissler,Khaled Kahouli,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReactionAtlas，一种基于机器学习的反应网络构建方法，能够从少量种子分子出发，通过生成模型提议反应、机器学习力场过滤过渡态，自动构建大规模化学反应网络。从8个前生物种子分子出发，发现了约47,000个反应和约12,000个化合物，实现了小分子碳水化合物化学的空前尺度和精度映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学反应网络（包含极小点、过渡态及基元反应）是化学的自然语言，但传统方法（如DFT）构建此类网络速度极慢且需要输入反应物和产物。缺乏一种能够从零开始、无需手工规则、高效构建大规模反应网络的方法。

Method: ReactionAtlas包含两个核心组件：(1) 机器学习的生成模型，从动力学采样的候选化合物中提议反应；(2) DFT训练的机器学习力场（MLFF），用于筛选有效的过渡态。生成的产物作为新的种子进入搜索，形成迭代循环。从8个前生物种子分子（CH₂O, H₂O, OH⁻, H₃O⁺, CO₂, H₂CO₃, HCO₃⁻, H）出发进行构建。

Result: 从8个种子分子出发，ReactionAtlas发现了约47,000个反应和约12,000个化合物。MLFF预测的过渡态与PBE0参考值的RMSD在0.5 Å以内的占85%，并可轻松提升至PBE0精度。映射了小分子碳水化合物化学（最高C₄H₈O₄），包含电荷和立体化学信息。

Conclusion: ReactionAtlas以前所未有的规模和精度实现了化学反应网络的自动构建，能够对甲酸循环等经典反应路径提供新的见解，并发现替代反应路径，对化学起源研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Mapping a chemical reaction network, the graph of minima and transition states (TS) and the elementary reactions connecting them, is the natural language of chemistry, from catalysis to combustion to the origin of life. Constructing such a reaction network for a given chemistry has been impractical: it requires finding and characterizing tens of thousands of TS, a task for which traditional methods such as density functional theory (DFT) are typically prohibitively slow and require reactant and product as input. We introduce ReactionAtlas, which builds a reaction network $\textit{ab origine}$ from a handful of seed molecules and without hand-crafted rules. Specifically, our machine-learned generative model proposes reactions from kinetically sampled candidate compounds and a DFT-trained machine learned force field (MLFF) filters them to valid TS, the resulting products of which enter the search as new seeds. Starting from eight pre-biotic seeds (CH$_2$O, H$_2$O, OH$^-$, H$_3$O$^+$, CO$_2$, H$_2$CO$_3$, HCO$_3^-$, H), ReactionAtlas discovers $\sim$47,000 reactions among $\sim$12,000 compounds. The MLFF TSs match the PBE0 references within 0.5 Å RMSD in 85% of the cases and can be easily brought to the PBE0 level. Thus, ReactionAtlas maps small carbohydrate chemistry up to C$_4$H$_8$O$_4$ at unprecedented scale and accuracy, including charge and stereo information. It enables novel insights into many well-studied reaction paths, including the formose cycle, which we highlight for its centrality to the chemical origins of life. Notably, our framework also allows establishing alternative reaction pathways for formose chemistry.

</details>


### [64] [Revocable Learned State via Process Sidecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30788)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“过程副驾驶”（process sidecars）方法，通过两系数编辑族解决语言模型在分阶段训练（技能→记忆→安全）后，因安全优化导致记忆方向被“运输”而无法简单撤回的问题。该方法利用安全训练过程的雅可比信息构建二阶精确的编辑，证明优于传统任务算术编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常分阶段训练：先学习技能和记忆，再进行安全对齐。然而，安全阶段的优化器会“弯曲”或“运输”之前写入的记忆方向，导致事后简单减去记忆更新无法恢复安全模型的状态。现有任务算术编辑方法忽略了这个过程信息，存在一阶误差。作者旨在提出一种能够补偿安全训练过程对记忆方向影响的编辑方法。

Method: 提出过程副驾驶（process sidecars）编辑族：$\hatθ(λ,γ)=θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}-λΔ_{\mathrm{M}}-γ\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$，其中$\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}=\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}(Δ_{\mathrm{M}})-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$，$\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}$是通过在$θ_{\mathrm{A}}-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$处计算一次额外安全训练轨迹得到的中心割线近似。取$\varepsilon=1$，复用$θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}$作为正端点。理论上证明：使用真实运输方向$R_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$的精确副驾驶在$(λ,γ)=(1,1)$处以二阶精度恢复反事实安全只训练模型$θ_{\mathrm{AS}}$；且当安全训练弯曲记忆方向时，任何标量任务算术编辑都会留下二阶误差而副驾驶保持二阶精度。

Result: 在三个模型上，经过验证集选择的二维编辑在所有试验中均优于朴素任务算术的保留拒绝闭包（held-out refusal closure），并且在所有配对试验中优于$\gamma=\lambda$的过程JVP子族（缓存二维网格的对角切片）。

Conclusion: 过程副驾驶方法通过显式建模安全训练对记忆方向的运输效应，实现了比传统任务算术更精确的模型编辑。理论分析和实验验证均表明，利用安全训练过程的雅可比信息是必要的，能够将编辑精度从一阶提升到二阶。

Abstract: Language models are often adapted in stages: a public skill phase, a private memory phase, and a later safety phase that learns to refuse outputs tied to the remembered entities. Revoking the memory after the safety phase is not the same problem as subtracting the memory update: the later safety optimizer has transported the memory direction. We introduce process sidecars, a two-coefficient edit family $\hatθ(λ,γ)=θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}-λΔ_{\mathrm{M}}-γ\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$, with $\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}=\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}(Δ_{\mathrm{M}})-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$, where $\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}$ is a centered secant through the realized future AdamW safety-training process. The implementation uses $\varepsilon=1$ at the natural memory-edit scale; it reuses $θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}$ as the positive endpoint and computes one additional safety trace at $θ_{\mathrm{A}}-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$. We prove two things. First, the exact sidecar, using the true transported direction $R_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$ rather than the secant estimate, at $(λ,γ)=(1,1)$ recovers the counterfactual safety-only oracle $θ_{\mathrm{AS}}$ up to second order; the proof treats AdamW as an augmented-state map over parameters, first moments, and second moments. Second, this process information is necessary: whenever future safety training bends the memory direction, every scalar task-arithmetic edit leaves first-order counterfactual error, while the process-sidecar edit is second-order accurate. Across three models, the validation-selected 2D edit improves held-out refusal closure over naive task arithmetic in all trials, and over the $γ=λ$ process-JVP subfamily, the diagonal slice of the cached 2D grid, in all paired trials.

</details>


### [65] [Predictable GRPO: A Closed-Form Model of Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30789)
*Rajat Ghosh,Datta Nimmaturi,Aryan Singhal,Vaishnavi Bhargava,Henry Wong,Johnu George,Debojyoti Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRPO训练动力学的第一性原理降阶模型，揭示了其经验单指数饱和律的过阻尼极限本质，预测了群规模不变性、稳定性阈值和过阻尼-振荡相变，并提供了区分奖励黑客、优势退化、策略集中和动态不稳定性等失败模式的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的标准工具，但其训练动态仅停留在经验描述层面：奖励轨迹用低参数函数拟合（常数无机制意义），超参数选择依赖试错。缺乏从第一性原理出发的理论理解。

Method: 构建GRPO动力学的第一性原理降阶模型，引入惯性项修正传统单指数模型，推导出群规模不变性、稳定性阈值和过阻尼-振荡相变等可检验预测，并设计区分不同失败模式的诊断方法。

Result: 闭式轨迹拟合训练奖励达到R²≥0.91；预测的群规模不变性在奖励曲线和8个数学基准的分布外迁移上均成立；软最大化bandit约化实验复现了过阻尼-振荡相变，并将稳定性阈值定位在独立测量的刚度值处。

Conclusion: 该模型为GRPO训练动态提供了具有机制解释力的理论框架，将经验参数转化为具有物理意义的量，并为训练监控和故障诊断提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a standard tool for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, yet its training dynamics are still described empirically: reward trajectories are fit with low-parameter functional forms whose constants carry no mechanistic meaning, and hyperparameter choices remain a matter of trial and error.  We develop a first-principles reduced-order model of these dynamics. The reduction has three consequences. First, it subsumes the empirical single-exponential saturation law as its overdamped limit, recasting the fitted plateau, timescale, and size exponent as the fixed point, inverse stiffness, and curvature-scaling exponent of the underlying potential, and adding, through the retained inertial term, the slow-start phase the single exponential cannot represent. Second, it yields predictions tied to independently measurable quantities rather than fitted ones: group-size invariance of the deterministic trajectory with a $1/G$ stationary fluctuation, a sharp stability threshold in the refresh interval, and an overdamped-to-oscillatory transition. Third, it furnishes diagnostics that separate failure modes a reward curve alone conflates -- reward hacking, advantage degeneracy, policy concentration, and dynamical instability. Across three models and two group sizes, the closed-form trajectory fits training reward to $R^2 \geq 0.91$ and the predicted group-size invariance holds on both the reward curve and out-of-distribution transfer to eight math benchmarks. The stability and oscillatory predictions are exercised in a controlled exact-reduction setting where the mean-field assumption holds exactly: a softmax-bandit reduction reproduces the predicted overdamped-to-oscillatory transition and locates the refresh-interval stability threshold at the independently measured stiffness, with a deep-network demonstration left to future work.

</details>


### [66] [Gradient Smoothing: Coupling Layer-wise Updates for Improved Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30813)
*Haoming Meng,Anton Sugolov,Vardan Papyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度梯度增强（Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation）优化框架，并实例化为梯度平滑（Gradient Smoothing）方法，通过沿深度维度对块级优化器更新进行平滑变换，在不修改模型架构或训练目标的情况下，显著提升多种架构（Transformer、Vision Transformer等）和训练任务（语言模型预训练、RL后训练、扩散模型、图像分类）的优化与泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中重复架构块（如Transformer）的层间存在结构化关系，这种跨层结构在训练中自然涌现。作者希望利用这一观察，提出一种通用的优化范式来挖掘深度方向的结构信息，从而改进优化效果和泛化能力。

Method: 提出Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation框架，其核心是沿深度维度对块级优化器更新进行变换。具体实例化为梯度平滑（Gradient Smoothing）方法，使用局部窗口平滑（Window Smoothing）算子作用于任意基优化器（如SGD、Adam、Muon）产生的块级更新，计算开销极小且兼容现有优化流程。

Result: 在语言模型预训练、LLM推理的RL后训练、扩散模型、Vision Transformers图像分类等多种场景下，梯度平滑一致性地提升了优化和泛化性能，无需修改模型架构或训练目标。此外，该方法促进了深度方向上更结构化的表征演化。

Conclusion: 深度梯度增强是一个有前景的利用跨深度结构信息的优化框架，梯度平滑作为其简单且广泛适用的实例化方法，能有效提升多种深度学习任务的训练效果。

Abstract: Deep neural networks with repeated architectural blocks, such as transformers, often exhibit structured relationships across layers that emerge during training. Motivated by this observation, we introduce \emph{Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation}, a general optimization paradigm in which the update applied to each layer is obtained by transforming the collection of block-wise optimizer updates along the depth dimension. Within this framework, we study \emph{Gradient Smoothing}, a family of depth-wise smoothing methods, and instantiate it with a simple local \emph{Window Smoothing} operator. The resulting method operates directly on block-wise updates produced by arbitrary base optimizers (e.g., SGD, Adam, Muon), incurs minimal computational overhead, and is compatible with existing optimization pipelines. We evaluate Gradient Smoothing across a diverse set of architectures and training regimes, including language model pretraining, RL post-training of LLMs for reasoning, diffusion modeling, and image classification with Vision Transformers. Across these settings, Gradient Smoothing consistently improves optimization and generalization performance without modifying model architectures or training objectives. We further show that it promotes more structured representation evolution across depth, consistent with its interpretation as a structured depth-wise preconditioning method. Together, these results establish Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation as a promising framework for exploiting cross-depth structure in optimization and demonstrate Gradient Smoothing as a simple and broadly applicable instantiation.

</details>


### [67] [Mind the Residual Gap: Probabilistic Downscaling under Real-World Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30821)
*Yujin Kim,Nidhi Soma,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对概率降尺度任务中均值-残差框架存在的偏差和欠分散问题，指出根本原因是残差目标错配（训练与测试时的残差分布不一致），并提出ReMatch方法，通过最优传输在低维PCA空间中对齐训练残差分布与测试分布，有效改善校准性能并在合成基准和真实HRRR-ERA5风场降尺度任务中超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率降尺度任务中广泛使用的均值-残差框架在实际应用中频繁产生有偏和欠分散的集合，现有研究将其归结为通用的预测不确定性校准问题，而作者认为根本原因更为本质——残差目标错配，即训练时诱导的残差分布与测试时所需的残差分布因降尺度偏差而系统性地不同。

Method: 提出ReMatch方法，通过低维PCA空间中的最优传输（Optimal Transport）对齐训练残差分布与测试时的目标分布，从而减少随机生成器所见残差目标的训练-测试分布不匹配，保留均值-残差框架的统计优势同时修正错配问题。

Result: 在具有不同偏差水平的可控合成基准和真实HRRR-ERA5风场降尺度任务上，ReMatch显著减少了欠分散问题，改善了校准指标（SSR和CRPS），并超越了标准均值-残差模型及其变体以及最先进的超分辨率模型。

Conclusion: 残差目标错配是均值-残差框架在实际应用中失效的根本原因，ReMatch通过分布对齐有效解决了这一问题，为概率降尺度提供了一种更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic downscaling is the task of modeling the conditional distribution of high-resolution fields given coarse inputs, and is a central challenge to atmospheric science, climate modeling, and other multiscale physical systems. A widely used paradigm decomposes the problem into a deterministic mean predictor followed by a stochastic residual generator. While effective in idealized settings, this mean--residual approach frequently produces biased and under-dispersive ensembles in real-world applications. Is this merely generic predictive uncertainty miscalibration? We show that the root cause is more fundamental: residual target misspecification, the residual distribution induced during training differs systematically from the one required at test time due to downscaling bias. To close this gap, we introduce ReMatch (Residual Distribution Matching). ReMatch aligns the training residual distribution toward the test-time regime via optimal transport in a low-dimensional PCA space. This preserves the statistical benefits of the mean--residual framework while reducing the train--test mismatch in the residual targets seen by the stochastic generator. On a controlled synthetic benchmark with varying bias levels and a real-world HRRR--ERA5 wind field downscaling task, ReMatch substantially reduces under-dispersion, improves calibration (SSR and CRPS), and outperforms strong baselines, including the standard mean--residual model and its variants, as well as state-of-the-art super-resolution models. Our code is available at https://github.com/sdean-group/ReMatch.git.

</details>


### [68] [Partition-Guided Distance Saliency: Bridging Decision and Objective Spaces in Many-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30836)
*Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes,Flávio Vinícius Cruzeiro Martins,Elizabeth Fialho Wanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多目标优化中高维Pareto前沿可解释性差的问题，提出PGDS框架，通过代理模型、自动分区和距离灵敏度分析三阶段流水线，将决策变量分类为"驱动者"或"阻碍者"，在10目标基准和工程问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化(MaO)中随着目标数量增加，帕累托前沿复杂度急剧上升，高维决策变量与目标结果之间的关系变得不透明；传统可视化方法无法处理高维目标空间，决策者缺乏先验知识时难以识别相关权衡或指定目标区域，存在"认知干旱"问题。

Method: 提出PGDS框架，包含三个阶段：(1)构建代理模型学习决策空间几何距离到目标空间邻近度的映射；(2)自动划分目标景观为不同区域，识别局部"支配点"作为自动目标；(3)通过扰动每个决策变量测量距离偏移，量化解对各变量的敏感性，将特征分为"驱动者"(促进收敛)和"阻碍者"(限制进展的几何约束)。

Result: 在10目标基准测试和物理信息工程问题(Welded Beam)上验证，PGDS提供了差异化的、可操作的洞察，优于传统可视化和基于规则的XAI方法。

Conclusion: PGDS通过几何直觉驱动的三阶段流水线，有效弥合了高维多目标优化的可解释性鸿沟，为决策者提供了明确可操作的特征归类（驱动者/阻碍者），在复杂优化场景中有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Explainability in Many-Objective Optimization (MaO) is currently hindered by the escalating complexity of the Pareto front, which renders the relationship between high-dimensional decision variables and objective outcomes increasingly opaque. As the number of objectives exceeds the limits of traditional visualization, decision-makers encounter a ``cognitive drought'' in identifying relevant trade-offs or specifying target regions without a priori knowledge. To bridge this interpretability gap, we introduce the {Partition-Guided Distance Saliency (PGDS)} framework, a novel XAI approach designed for continuous optimization landscapes. Our framework automates the explanation process through a three-stage pipeline that prioritizes geometric intuition over abstract rules. First, we employ a surrogate model that learns how geometric distances in the decision space map to proximity in the objective space. Second, to address the difficulty of manual target selection in high dimensions, the framework automatically partitions the objective landscape into distinct regions and identifies local ``Dominating Points'' to serve as automated targets for improvement. Third, we quantify how sensitive a solution's position is to each decision variable by measuring the distance shifts induced by perturbations to each variable. This allows PGDS to categorize features as either ``Drivers'' which facilitate convergence toward preferred regions, or ``Blockers'' which represent geometric constraints hindering further progress. Validation on 10-objective benchmarks and a physics-informed engineering problem (Welded Beam) demonstrates that PGDS provides differentiated, actionable insights that traditional visualization and rule-based XAI methods fail to provide.

</details>


### [69] [A Stationary-Distribution Theory for Triplet-Based Plateau Search in Random Forest Ensemble-Size Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30837)
*Andrey A. Dukhovny,Andrey M. Lange*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了随机森林中基于高原的树数量调优过程的平稳分布理论，将该过程建模为几何网格上的生灭马尔可夫链，推导了中心集成规模的平稳分布，并给出了平稳展宽的刻画，证明该过程应被视为随机过程而非确定性停止规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林中树的数量是一个关键计算参数，增加树的数量会减少有限集成的变异性，但会增加训练和预测成本。基于高原的调优方法通过局部比较袋外分数来调整树的数量，但剩余超参数稳定后，中心三元组点并不收敛到确定值，而是在平稳状态附近波动，需要理论解释这一现象。

Method: 将中心集成规模 B_t 建模为几何网格上的生灭马尔可夫链，通过局部平衡推导其平稳分布；采用中心折叠正态近似得到原始更新规则和对称修改变体的平衡方程；利用局部高斯近似和福克-普朗克解释获得网格级方差常数，再转换到集成规模尺度。

Result: 平稳中心 B_* = O(ε^{-2}) 当 ε↓0；平稳展宽方面，σ_{B,*} = O(ε^{-2})，方差为 O(ε^{-4})；前导相对展宽与 ε 无关，由尺度因子和更新规则控制。

Conclusion: 这些结果将基于高原的随机森林调优解释为随机过程而非确定性停止规则，为理解该方法的统计行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The number of trees is a central computational parameter in Random Forests: increasing it reduces finite-ensemble variability but increases training and prediction cost. Plateau-based tuning adapts this parameter through local comparisons of out-of-bag scores at a geometric triplet of tree counts. After the remaining hyperparameters have stabilized, however, the central triplet point need not converge to a deterministic value; instead, it fluctuates around a stationary regime.
  This paper develops a stationary-distribution theory for this process. The central ensemble size $B_t$ is modeled as a birth-death Markov chain on a geometric grid, and its stationary distribution is derived through local balance. Under a leading centered folded-normal approximation, equilibrium equations are obtained for the original update rule and a symmetric modified variant, implying that the stationary center $B_*=O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ as $\varepsilon\downarrow 0$.
  The stationary spread is also characterized. A local Gaussian approximation and a Fokker-Planck interpretation give grid-level variance constants. After conversion to the ensemble-size scale, $σ_{B,*}=O(\varepsilon^{-2})$, while the variance is $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$. The leading relative spread is independent of $\varepsilon$ and controlled by the scale factor and update rule. These results interpret plateau-based Random Forest tuning as a stochastic process rather than a deterministic stopping rule.

</details>


### [70] [A Transferable Learned Temporal Prior for Transmission Reconstruction and Decision-Relevant Uncertainty in Real Outbreak Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30842)
*Md Ahsan Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了疫情传播重建中流行病学时序和传播标签的不确定性。通过锁定11个疾病家族训练的logistic回归时序先验，在29个ANDV任务上达到MRR 0.571（vs基线0.274）和Top-1准确率37.9%（vs 13.8%）。对75对NYC mpox的基因组一致性审计发现54.67%的传播对未被基因组支持。保留不确定边会改变优先干预的源病例集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疫情传播重建方法将流行病学时序和传播标签当作确定性金标准，但这两者从未被系统评估过，可能导致干预优先级误判。

Method: 训练一个logistic回归时序先验（基于11个疾病家族），在29个ANDV父级排序任务上锁定参数进行零次微调评估；对75对NYC mpox进行基因组一致性审计作为独立标签可靠性证据。

Result: 时序先验MRR 0.571 vs 0.274，Top-1准确率37.9% vs 13.8%（p≤0.0002）；54.67%的mpox传播对基因组未解决或未支持；保留不确定边时ANDV和Delta图的top-5源优先级集合Jaccard系数仅0.429-0.667。

Conclusion: 传播标签的不确定性在疫情证据模块中可测量，保留不确定链接会改变优先干预的源病例选择，表明在疫情响应中应明确考虑此类不确定性。

Abstract: Outbreak transmission reconstruction treats epidemiological timing and transmission labels as deterministic ground truth; neither has been systematically evaluated. We trained a logistic regression temporal prior on eleven disease families, locked all parameters before accessing any target outbreak data, and applied it without refitting to a strict Andes virus (ANDV) parent-ranking benchmark of 29 tasks. The locked prior achieved mean reciprocal rank (MRR) 0.571 versus 0.274 and Top-1 accuracy 37.9% versus 13.8% against the best source-trained parametric baseline (permutation p <= 0.0002; 7-8 reversals to lose MRR significance). A phylogenetic concordance audit of 75 NYC mpox inter-host pairs - independent label-reliability evidence rather than a prior validation - found that 54.67% (exact 95% CI: 42.75-66.21%) were genomically unresolved or unsupported. Retaining uncertain edges in ANDV and Guangdong Delta graphs shifted top-5 source-priority sets (Jaccard 0.429-0.667). Transmission-label uncertainty was measurable in the outbreak evidence modules examined, and retaining uncertain links changed which source cases were prioritized for intervention.

</details>


### [71] [Behavior Cloning is Not All You Need: The Optimality of On-Policy Distillation for Noisy Expert Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30923)
*Ved Sriraman,Peihan Liu,Daniel Hsu,Adam Block*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个带噪声专家模型来解释为什么在线模仿学习（如OPD）通常优于离线方法（如SFT），理论证明在噪声专家设定下离线学习的样本复杂度呈指数增长，而在线方法可实现多项式复杂度，为语言模型训练中OPD优于SFT提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决模仿学习中理论与实践的矛盾：理论上离线IL可以达到无水平依赖且最优，但实际上在线方法（如on-policy distillation）往往优于离线方法（如SFT）。作者试图解释这一差距。

Method: 提出噪声专家模型（noisy expert model），其中学习者只能访问专家的带噪版本，但需要与干净专家竞争。通过理论分析证明离线与在线IL之间存在显著分离：离线学习噪声轨迹的样本复杂度呈指数增长，而在线交互（通过OPD变体）可实现多项式依赖。进一步在专家噪声分布满足特定条件时，可得到无水平依赖的保证。

Result: 1) 离线学习噪声专家轨迹本质困难，样本复杂度指数级增长；2) 在线交互可实现多项式水平依赖；3) 在专家噪声分布的自然条件下可实现无水平依赖的样本复杂度；4) 提出与语言模型训练中常用损失函数对应的替代损失函数；5) 将结果扩展到未知污染设定。

Conclusion: 为在线策略蒸馏（OPD）优于监督微调（SFT）提供了理论基础，解释了在训练语言模型（如长链思维推理）时，面对不完美教师，在线方法为何更有效。

Abstract: Imitation Learning is a natural framework for learning in sequential decision-making systems and has emerged as the dominant paradigm through which we understand language model training. A central puzzle is that, while in theory offline IL can be horizon-free and optimal, in practice online methods such as on-policy distillation often outperform offline methods such as supervised fine-tuning. We propose a noisy expert model to explain this gap, in which the learner only has access to a noisy version of the expert's policy, but wishes to compete against the reward achieved by a clean expert, motivated by the fact that in many applications, e.g. training language models to perform long chains of thought, the expert is often imperfect. In this setting, we show a sharp separation between offline and online IL. Offline learning from noisy trajectories is fundamentally hard: to compete with the clean expert, the sample complexity must grow exponentially, in contradistinction to the clean expert setting where no explicit horizon dependence exists. In contrast, we prove that online interaction with the noisy expert via a novel variant of OPD enables polynomial dependence on the horizon in general. We further show that, under a natural condition on the expert noise distribution, which we show to be necessary for any horizon-free sample complexity, one can obtain such a guarantee, although our proposed algorithm sacrifices statistical efficiency in its dependence on the size of the policy class. Our analysis leads to an alternative loss function that is commonly considered empirically for LM training. We further provide algorithms and lower bounds, and extend our results to the more realistic setting of unknown corruption when the clean expert is deterministic, thereby providing a theoretical foundation for why OPD can outperform SFT when training language models from imperfect teachers.

</details>


### [72] [Personalizing Marketplace Policies with Competing Objectives and Constrained Experiments: Evidence from a Job Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30932)
*Yufei Wu,Zhen Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成框架，用于在双边求职市场中个性化设置免费价值阈值政策，在满足参与度约束的同时显著提升目标指标。该框架包含集成混合排序模型、处理效应外推方法和生产部署，有效解决了跨边外部性和市场干扰带来的优化挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边市场中不同用户群体的利益往往冲突，提升一方体验可能损害另一方。标准抬升方法存在两个不足：一是跨边外部性需要多目标优化，最大化雇主侧指标会损害求职者参与度；二是市场干扰需要聚类级随机化，只能使用少数离散处理水平，排除了连续处理方法。

Method: 提出三组件集成框架：(1) 集成混合排序模型，分别建模目标和防护指标，相比单目标方法可将防护风险降低10%以上；(2) 处理效应外推方法，基于单调性假设将有限实验变化扩展到未测试的政策水平；(3) 生产部署，用上线数据验证外推精度和防护合规。

Result: 个性化策略在目标指标上带来了统计显著且经济上可观的提升，同时满足参与度约束。防护风险降低超过10%。上线数据证实了外推方法的准确性和防护指标的合规性。

Conclusion: 即使实验严重受限且目标相互竞争，原则性的方法论仍能实现有意义的个性化，这适用于许多真实世界中的市场平台。

Abstract: Two-sided marketplaces connect distinct user groups whose interests often conflict -- improving outcomes on one side could degrade the other side's experience. To address this challenge, we deploy an integrated framework for personalizing free-value thresholds -- a policy governing the scope of complimentary services for job listings -- across a two-sided job marketplace connecting millions of employers and job seekers. Our personalized policy delivers statistically significant and economically sizable lift in the target metric while respecting engagement guardrail constraints.
  Direct application of standard uplift methods proves insufficient here for two reasons. First, cross-side externalities demand multi-objective optimization: maximizing employer-side metrics risks harming job seeker engagement, with effects varying substantially across job segments. Second, marketplace interference necessitates cluster-level randomization, limiting us to few discrete treatment levels -- effectively a form of positivity violation that rules out methods designed for continuous treatments.
  We contribute an integrated framework with three components. Our ensemble-based hybrid ranking models target and guardrail metrics separately, cutting guardrail risk by over 10% for equivalent target gains compared to single-objective approaches. A treatment effect extrapolation method extends our estimates from limited experimental variation to untested policy levels, relying on monotonicity assumptions that we validate empirically. Finally, we present production deployment, where post-launch data confirms both extrapolation accuracy and guardrail compliance.
  Our deployed system demonstrates that principled methodology can enable meaningful personalization even when experiments are severely constrained and different objectives compete -- common conditions that characterize many real-world marketplaces.

</details>


### [73] [Quality-Aware Modulation for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30934)
*Luke Budny,Yuhong Guo,Kevin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出质量表示模块(QRM)，一种轻量级Transformer模块，通过学习质量感知表示并生成向量M_qrm，调整DiT中的自适应LayerNorm调制，向去噪参数注入质量敏感信号，从而在不改变采样调度或扩散主干的情况下提升图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像的扩散模型（如DiT）依赖时间步或提示嵌入来调节去噪强度，但这种调制仅传达当前噪声水平，缺乏质量感知信息，导致生成的图像存在不对齐、视觉不一致和保真度低的问题。

Method: 提出质量表示模块(QRM)，一种轻量级Transformer模块，基于现有模型输入学习质量感知表示并生成向量集合M_qrm，通过调整DiT Transformer块内的自适应LayerNorm调制，将质量敏感信号注入去噪参数，且不改变采样调度或扩散主干结构。

Result: 消融实验验证了QRM的训练损失和架构设计，实证结果表明QRM在DiT基模型上能持续提升图像质量。

Conclusion: QRM通过轻量级模块注入质量感知信息，在不改变基础架构的前提下有效提升了扩散模型的图像生成质量。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image diffusion models, such as diffusion transformers (DiT), rely on timestep or prompt embeddings to modulate the strength of the denoising process in each timestep.
