Our results establish pre-determined forking as a promising research direction for efficient LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [112] [RaBitQCache: Rotated Binary Quantization for KVCache in Long Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31519)
*Wenhao Li,Jinhao Dong,Hailin Zhang,Wenhang Shi,Wei Lu,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RaBitQCache提出了一种基于随机旋转二元量化与二元-INT4混合算术的新型稀疏注意力框架，通过无偏估计的代理分数实现自适应Top-p检索，并结合异步流水线与延迟更新等硬件感知优化，显著加速长上下文LLM推理并降低内存I/O，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型推理受限于巨大的键值缓存，现有稀疏注意力方法要么采用静态固定预算（Top-k）检索，要么依赖计算昂贵且有偏的代理分数，亟需一种高效且无偏的稀疏注意力机制。

Method: 利用随机旋转二元量化和高吞吐二元-INT4算术高效估计注意力权重；所提代理分数为无偏估计量且有理论误差界；基于此实现自适应Top-p检索以动态调整token预算；设计了包含异步流水线和延迟更新的硬件感知系统来掩盖开销。

Result: 在与最先进基线的对比评估中，RaBitQCache显著加速了推理并减少了内存I/O，同时保持了生成质量。

Conclusion: RaBitQCache通过无偏估计与自适应检索策略有效解决了长上下文推理中KV缓存瓶颈问题，在效率与质量之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Long-context Large Language Model inference is severely bottlenecked by the massive Key-Value (KV) cache, yet existing sparse attention methods often suffer from static fixed-budget (Top-k) retrieval or rely on proxy scores that are computationally expensive and biased. To address these limitations, we propose RaBitQCache, a novel sparse attention framework that utilizes randomized rotated binary quantization and high-throughput binary-INT4 arithmetic to efficiently estimate attention weights. Our proxy score serves as an unbiased estimator with a proven error bound, enabling adaptive Top-p retrieval that dynamically adjusts the token budget based on actual attention sparsity. We further implement a hardware-aware system with asynchronous pipelining and lazy updates to mask overhead. Evaluations demonstrate that RaBitQCache significantly accelerates inference and reduces memory I/O while preserving generation quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/Sakuraaa0/RaBitQCache.git.

</details>


### [113] [On the Convergence of Self-Improving Online LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31524)
*Xudong Wu,Pangpang Liu,Vaneet Aggarwal,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对SAIL算法在分布偏移对齐任务中缺乏收敛性分析的问题，本文提出SAIL-RevKL算法，通过引入逆向KL散度正则化项改善优化景观，证明了其在有界参数空间内满足PL条件，并建立了近乎线性的样本复杂度下的全局收敛保证。实验表明，SAIL-RevKL在MuJoCo基准和LLM对齐任务上均优于原始SAIL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAIL目标函数的海森矩阵性质不佳，无法保证强凹性，导致其收敛性质缺乏形式化分析。

Method: 提出SAIL-RevKL算法，在原始目标函数中引入逆向KL散度正则化项，将双层优化问题简化为高效的单层方法，并证明该正则化目标在有界参数空间内满足Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL)条件。

Result: 建立了全局收敛保证，实现了近乎线性的样本复杂度；在MuJoCo基准和LLM对齐任务上，SAIL-RevKL优于原始SAIL。

Conclusion: 逆向KL散度正则化有效改善了SAIL算法的优化景观，解决了其理论收敛性分析的空白，同时提升了实际性能。

Abstract: The Self-Improving Alignment (SAIL) algorithm addresses distribution shift by reducing a bilevel formulation of the problem to an efficient, single-level method. Empirically, SAIL has demonstrated strong performance on this task. However, a formal analysis of its convergence properties has been lacking. We identify a key theoretical challenge: the standard SAIL objective function is not guaranteed to be strongly concave due to unfavorable properties of its Hessian. To address this limitation, we propose a regularized objective, SAIL-RevKL, which incorporates a reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence penalty to improve the optimization landscape. Our central theoretical contribution is to prove that this regularized objective satisfies the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition within a bounded parameter space. We establish global convergence guarantees, achieving a near-linear sample complexity. We further validate the effectiveness and stability of SAIL-RevKL through empirical evaluations, demonstrating that it outperforms the vanilla SAIL on both MuJoCo benchmarks and LLM alignment tasks.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond the Expressivity-Trainability Paradox: A Dynamical Lie Algebra Perspective on Navigating Barren Plateaus in Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31536)
*Kung-Ming Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量子机器学习中一个反直觉的现象：无结构化的参数化量子电路（PQC）因其巨大的希尔伯特空间容量而导致“表达性-可训练性悖论”，即量子欠拟合问题。作者通过动态李代数（DLA）和几何QML框架，证明了无结构架构会遭遇梯度消失的贫瘠高原（BP），而引入群论几何先验作为结构正则化器，通过限制DLA多项式增长，在牺牲记忆容量的同时保证了可扩展的富梯度训练景观，实现了“可训练性设计”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计学习理论中增加模型容量会导致过拟合，但无结构的QML架构却面临量子欠拟合问题，其根源在于“表达性-可训练性悖论”：巨大的希尔伯特空间容量虽然被视为量子优势的来源，却直接导致贫瘠高原（梯度指数级消失）。需要新的理论框架来理解并解决这一矛盾。

Method: 综合动态李代数（DLA）和几何QML的理论成果，建立电路生成器代数维度与优化动力学之间的联系；在非线性二分类任务上实证验证该框架，对比无结构架构（通过不可扩展参数化实现近乎完美的训练精度，即量子过拟合）与加入群论几何先验的结构正则化方法。

Result: 无结构量子架构存在独特的偏置-方差权衡：一方面导致量子过拟合（通过不可扩展参数化实现高训练精度），另一方面面临量子欠拟合（贫瘠高原导致的不可训练性）。对称性保持方法通过将DLA增长限制在多项式范围内，以牺牲原始记忆容量为代价，保证可扩展的富梯度训练景观。

Conclusion: 为可扩展的量子神经网络提供了“可训练性设计”（Trainability-by-Design）的鲁棒路线图，指出几何先验作为结构正则化器是解决QML可训练性问题的关键方向。

Abstract: As Quantum Machine Learning (QML) transitions toward practical implementation, the field faces a critical architectural bottleneck that challenges the fundamental assumptions of classical statistical learning theory. In classical deep learning, increasing model capacity typically risks overfitting. However, this study advances a counter-intuitive paradigm: unstructured contemporary QML architectures suffer from a profound state of quantum underfitting, driven by the "expressivity-trainability paradox." We demonstrate that the vast Hilbert space capacity of Parameterized Quantum Circuits (PQCs)-traditionally chased as the source of quantum advantage is the direct mathematical cause of Barren Plateaus (BPs), where gradient landscapes become exponentially flat. By synthesizing recent breakthroughs in Dynamical Lie Algebras (DLAs) and Geometric QML, we establish a comprehensive framework linking the algebraic dimension of circuit generators to their optimization dynamics. Furthermore, we empirically validate this framework on a non-linear binary classification task, illuminating a uniquely quantum manifestation of the bias-variance tradeoff: while unstructured architectures achieve near-perfect training accuracy via unscalable parameterization (quantum overfitting), embedding group-theoretic geometric priors acts as a structural regularizer. By restricting the DLA growth to a polynomial regime, our symmetry-preserving approach sacrifices raw memorization capacity to guarantee scalable, gradient-rich training landscapes, offering a robust roadmap for "Trainability-by-Design" in scalable quantum neural networks.

</details>


### [115] [Introduction to Stochastic Differential Equations for Generative Machine Learning: A Variational Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31576)
*Ole Winther,Paul Jeha,Sander Dieleman,Andriy Mnih,Manfred Opper,Andrea Dittadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提供了关于常微分方程和随机微分方程在生成式机器学习中应用的入门介绍，重点讨论了扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配方法，并将它们统一为变分方法的特定参数化形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为生成式机器学习中使用的微分方程、概率框架和Fokker-Planck方程提供一个自包含且非正式的介绍，并将扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配统一到变分框架下。

Method: 从变分下界（ELBO）出发，将扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配视为最一般变分方法的特定参数化形式，并通过一维密度建模问题比较不同参数化方法。

Result: 成功将扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配统一到变分框架下，并通过一维示例展示了不同参数化的比较。

Conclusion: 所有生成方法（扩散模型、分数匹配、流匹配）可被视为变分方法的不同参数化，Fokker-Planck方程描述了随机变量边际分布的时序演化。

Abstract: The use of ordinary and stochastic differential equations has led to substantial progress in generative machine learning with applications to, for example, image, video and biomolecule generation. This paper provides a self-contained and informal introduction to the differential equations, the probabilistic framework for using them in generative modeling and the Fokker--Planck equation that governs the temporal evolution of the marginal distribution of the stochastic variables of the differential equations. The variational lower bound on the log-likelihood (the evidence lower bound, ELBO) is derived and used as a general starting point for a discussion of diffusion models, score matching, and flow matching. All of these approaches may be viewed as specific parameterizations of the most general variational approach. A one-dimensional density modeling problem is used as a simple example to compare different parameterizations.

</details>


### [116] [Robustness of neural networks to random noise perturbations of their inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31581)
*Mark Levene,Martyn Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究训练好的神经网络对输入扰动的鲁棒性，提出了一个简单高效的鲁棒性度量方法，该方法以高概率给出在输入扰动下网络均方误差的上界，并通过鲁棒性曲线进一步分析数据集内外的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在实际应用中常面临输入扰动（如噪声、对抗性攻击），因此需要量化网络对扰动的鲁棒性，并理解鲁棒性与准确性之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出一种将神经网络视为黑盒的鲁棒性度量方法，以高概率给出输入扰动下网络均方误差的上界；同时引入鲁棒性曲线概念，用于分析数据集内部及跨数据集的鲁棒性特征。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒性度量既简单又高效，可有效评估神经网络对输入扰动的鲁棒性，鲁棒性曲线为进一步分析提供了有用工具。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of the robustness of a trained neural network to the perturbation of its input values. More specifically, we examine the interplay between the accuracy of the network, as measured by the mean squared error, and robustness. Accordingly, we present a robustness measure, which, with high probability, suggests an upper bound on the mean squared error of the network, with respect to an input data set, for a given perturbation of the input values of the network. The measure we propose is both simple and efficient to compute, treating the neural network as a black box. We provide experimental results on several real-world data sets showing the efficacy of the proposed method. We also introduce the concept of robustness curves, which allows us to further analyse robustness within and between data sets.

</details>


### [117] [Evil Spectra: How Optimisers can Amplify or Suppress Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31591)
*Jason R. Brown,Patrick Leask,Lev McKinney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型（LLM）中“涌现性错位”（EM）现象对训练选择（特别是优化器）的敏感性。研究发现优化器选择是影响EM严重程度的最关键因素，可产生7倍的错位率差异；模型规模（1B-235B）和家族影响可忽略。Muon优化器通过隐式正则化LoRA适配器的奇异值分布来最好地保持对齐，而基于奇异值谱平坦化的额外正则化能显著恢复Adam和Lion等易错位优化器的对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前的研究发现LLM在窄领域错位任务上微调会导致广泛的错位行为，且错位严重程度对训练选择高度敏感，但缺乏对这种敏感性的系统性表征。本文旨在系统识别影响EM严重程度的关键训练因素，并探索缓解策略。

Method: 在Qwen3等12个模型上进行系统扫描，考察优化器、模型规模、数据集、批量大小等因素对错位率的影响；分析损失-对齐关系；通过奇异值谱正则化方法测试对齐恢复效果。

Result: 优化器选择产生7倍错位率差异，是最大影响因素；模型规模（1B-235B）和家族影响可忽略；最终对数训练损失是对齐的强预测因子；Muon优化器通过隐式正则化奇异值分布最好地保持对齐；基于奇异值谱平坦化的额外损失项能显著恢复Adam和Lion的对齐性能。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是EM严重程度的关键因素，但基于奇异值谱的谱正则化可以有效缓解易错位优化器的EM效应，且几乎不影响训练损失。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is a recently discovered phenomenon in LLMs where fine-tuning on a narrow misaligned task, such as writing insecure code, leads to broadly misaligned behaviour on unrelated prompts. Previous work has noted that the severity of EM is highly sensitive to training choices; however, we still lack a systematic characterisation of this sensitivity. We perform a sweep over several Qwen3 models, optimisers, datasets, and batch sizes, and find that the choice of optimiser has the largest effect, producing a 7x spread in misalignment rate. Surprisingly, model size has a negligible effect within the Qwen3 family. An additional sweep over 12 models from three families using Adam confirms that model scale (1B-235B) and family have negligible effects for that optimiser. Analysing the loss-alignment relationship on Qwen3-8B, we find that final log training loss is a strong predictor of alignment, and that stratifying by optimiser captures nearly all the residual variance. Training dynamics reveal that each optimiser follows a different trajectory through loss-alignment space, and that after significant training, the optimiser becomes more important than training loss as a predictor of alignment. Muon, the adaptive optimiser that preserves alignment the best, implicitly regularises for a more uniform distribution of singular values of the LoRA adapter. We evaluate this insight by training with an additional loss term that incentivises a flatter singular value spectrum, and find that this substantially recovers alignment for the more EM-prone adaptive optimisers (Adam and Lion), with negligible cost to training loss. These results identify optimiser choice as a key factor in EM severity, but show that spectral regularisation can substantially mitigate the effects of EM-prone optimisers.

</details>


### [118] [Calibration, Not Compilation: Detecting and Repairing Misspecified Probabilistic Programs Written by Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31630)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将贝叶斯工作流（后验预测检查、模拟校准、采样诊断等）作为概率程序的验证工具，而非传统的单元测试。研究表明，校准方法在检测统计错误（AUC 0.97）、指导LLM修复（GPT-5.1从33%提升至92%）以及评估真实世界代码（15-47%存在统计错误）方面均显著优于单元测试，甚至单元测试会因虚假自信抑制修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率程序即使通过编译和单元测试，仍可能存在统计错误（如似然函数选择不当、先验支持无效等）。传统的单元测试无法检测这些“统计错误”，因此需要更合适的验证工具。

Method: 定义一个“校准预言”（calibration oracle），包含后验预测检查、模拟校准、采样诊断（R-hat、发散、ESS）和留出预测密度。从三个维度评估：检测（14种错误类型×10个模型家族，200个实例）、修复（LLM循环修复）、现实性（LLM从零编写的程序）。

Result: 检测：校准预言AUC 0.97（2%假阳性率下88%检出率）；无参考版本达62-78%，单元测试为0%。修复：校准显著优于单元测试，单元测试甚至比无反馈更差（虚假自信）。现实：15-47%可运行程序存在统计错误，校准引导修复优于LLM评判、贝叶斯工作流检查表和数据摘要自调试。

Conclusion: 对于概率程序，正确性在于校准而非编译。单元测试不仅无法检测统计错误，还可能因虚假自信阻碍修复。贝叶斯工作流方法才是验证概率程序的正确范式。

Abstract: Language models increasingly write probabilistic programs (in NumPyro, Stan, or Pyro), but a program that compiles, runs, and passes every unit test can still be \emph{statistically} wrong -- a Gaussian likelihood for heavy-tailed data, a Poisson for over-dispersed counts, an invalid prior support, or a pathological parameterization. The right verifier is therefore not a test suite but the Bayesian workflow itself: posterior predictive checks, simulation-based calibration, sampler diagnostics ($\hat R$, divergences, ESS), and held-out predictive density. We study this calibration oracle along three axes. \textbf{Detection:} on a benchmark of $14$ misspecification types across $10$ model families ($200$ instances), it flags the bug with AUC $0.97$ ($88\%$ at $2\%$ FPR \emph{when handed the correct reference program, an upper bound}) -- and a fully \emph{reference-free} version that uses no correct program reaches $62$--$78\%$ (the upper figure from a small automated model search), versus $0\%$ for a unit-test oracle. \textbf{Repair:} used as feedback in an LLM repair loop across fifteen models, calibration significantly outperforms unit-test feedback -- which is itself \emph{significantly worse than no feedback at all}, a passing test inducing false confidence that suppresses repair -- and improves over no feedback on strong-but-unsaturated models (GPT-5.1 $33{\to}92\%$, Claude $75{\to}100\%$; paired McNemar, $n{=}228$). \textbf{Reality:} on programs LLMs write from scratch for neutral briefs, $15$--$47\%$ of runnable ones are statistically misspecified (unit tests catch none), and calibration-guided repair significantly beats LLM-as-judge review, a Bayesian-workflow checklist, and data-summary self-debug. Across all three, the lesson is the same: for probabilistic programs, correctness is calibration, not compilation.

</details>


### [119] [ECHO: Prune to act, trace to learn with selective turn memory in agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31650)
*Zijun Xie,Binbin Zheng,Enlei Gong,Jihua Liu,Yuyang You,Lingfeng Liu,Jiayao Tang,Guanqun Zhao,Aoqi Hu,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ECHO是一种选择性回合记忆框架，通过源索引重建解决了长程语言智能体在有限上下文窗口下的历史崩塌和可溯源学习问题，在BrowseComp-Plus上达到43.4%的准确率，优于GRPO（28.9%）和SUPO（36.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程上下文管理方法（截断、摘要、记忆选择）存在两个耦合限制：1）随着交互轮次增长，细粒度历史证据被逐步移除或压缩；2）原始回合失去源地址后，基于结果的强化学习无法将策略更新与支持成功答案的证据对齐。

Method: ECHO框架包含三个核心组件：1）将每个完成的环境回合压缩为紧凑的记忆记录；2）通过从这些记录中选择来重建有界策略上下文；3）利用选中的源索引将正向结果信用路由到支持成功答案的证据和选择动作上。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上，ECHO达到43.4%的留出准确率，超越GRPO（28.9%）和SUPO（36.1%），且使用更少的轮次和更低的轨迹量。训练的策略在多种骨干网络（密集和MoE）上展现出零样本泛化能力，涵盖多目标QA、代码生成和深度信息检索任务。

Conclusion: ECHO通过源索引重建有效缓解了历史崩塌问题，并为基于结果的RL提供了显式的信用分配路径，在长程智能体任务中实现了更高效、可溯源的策略学习。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents must repeatedly interact with tools, accumulate evidence, and make decisions under bounded context windows. Existing context-management methods make such rollouts feasible by truncating distant history, folding past turns into summaries, or selecting compact memory states. However, these breakthroughs introduce two coupled limitations. First, as the number of turns grows, historical observations are progressively removed or collapsed into compressed states, making it harder for the policy to reuse fine-grained evidence. Second, once the original turns are no longer source-addressable, outcome-based RL loses an explicit path for aligning policy updates with the evidence that supported a successful final answer. To this end, we propose ECHO, a selective turn-memory framework that jointly addresses history collapse and traceable learning through source-indexed reconstruction. Specifically, ECHO compresses each completed environment turn into a compact memory record, reconstructs bounded policy contexts by selecting from these records, and reuses the selected source indices to route positive outcome credit to the evidence and selection actions that support successful answers. On BrowseComp-Plus, ECHO reaches 43.4% held-out accuracy, outperforming GRPO (28.9%) and the rolling-summary baseline SUPO (36.1%), while using fewer turns and lower trajectory volume than SUPO (Figure 1). Additionally, the trained policy improves zero-shot generalization across multi-objective QA, code generation, and deep information-seeking benchmarks on both dense and MoE backbones.

</details>


### [120] [Improving Certified Robustness via Adversarial Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31653)
*Matteo Melis,Jesus Martinez Del Rincon,Vishal Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AD-CERT，一种结合对抗蒸馏和区间边界传播（IBP）上界的认证训练方法，通过在logit空间从经验鲁棒的教师模型蒸馏对抗信息，作为认证训练的有效下界代理，在多个鲁棒性基准上达到最优的认证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证训练方法存在标准精度与认证精度之间的权衡：基于紧松弛边界的方法牺牲标准精度，而对抗训练虽然经验鲁棒性强但难以形式化认证。已有工作表明结合对抗训练目标与松散的IBP上界可以改善这种权衡，本文旨在进一步利用对抗蒸馏来提升认证训练的性能。

Method: 提出AD-CERT目标函数，将对抗蒸馏与IBP上界结合，在logit空间从经验鲁棒的教师模型蒸馏对抗信息，作为认证训练中最坏情况损失的下界代理。在统一框架下与基于特征空间的鲁棒蒸馏目标进行对比。

Result: AD-CERT在多个鲁棒性基准上达到了最先进的认证性能。在统一设置下，logit级别的对抗蒸馏相比基于鲁棒特征空间的蒸馏目标，认证精度提升了高达5.40个百分点。

Conclusion: 对抗蒸馏信息（特别是在logit空间）可以有效地作为认证训练的下界代理，显著改善标准精度与认证精度之间的权衡。

Abstract: Certified training aims to produce models whose predictions can be formally verified against adversarial perturbations, typically by optimising upper bounds on the worst-case loss over an allowed perturbation set. For neural networks, certified training methods based purely on tight relaxation bounds produce networks that are amenable to certification, but sacrifice standard accuracy. Conversely, adversarial training often yields stronger empirical robustness and standard accuracy, but the resulting models are generally difficult to certify with neural network verifiers. Recently, the literature has shown that better standard-certified accuracy trade-offs can be achieved by combining adversarial training objectives with loose over-approximations based on Interval Bound Propagation (IBP), effectively interpolating between lower and upper bounds of the worst-case loss. Building on this, we introduce AD-CERT, a certified training objective that combines adversarial distillation with an IBP upper bound. We show that distilling adversarial information over the logit space from an empirically robust teacher provides an effective lower bound surrogate for certified training, with AD-CERT achieving state-of-the-art certified performance on several robustness benchmarks. Furthermore, in a unified setup, distilling adversarial information at the logit-level is shown to improve certified accuracy over a robust feature-space distillation objective by up to 5.40 percentage points.

</details>


### [121] [When to Truncate a Feature Ranking: A Residual-Overlap Stopping Rule for Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31686)
*Jesus S. Aguilar-Ruiz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于风险校准的分布框架，通过Bhattacharyya系数衡量类别间边际分离度，并设计残差乘积重叠停止规则，将监督特征排序转化为类别无关的全局特征子集，在高维基因组数据上实现从数万特征缩减至几十个且性能不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督特征选择中，特征排序后的截断步骤缺乏直接可解释的理论依据，通常依赖任意基数、经验阈值或交叉验证，无法回答"何时累积的类别分离证据足以停止选择特征"这一基本问题。

Method: 构建分布框架：1) 用Bhattacharyya系数度量每对类间条件分布的边际分离度；2) 对所有类别保留特征排序的最短前缀，使得每个相关类对比的残差乘积重叠低于预设阈值；3) 推导二元与多类贝叶斯风险界，提供先验依赖和先验自由的阈值校准方法。

Result: 在高维基因组数据集上，该方法可将数万特征缩减到几十个，同时保持与全特征基线统计相当的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该停止规则仅需一维边际重叠估计并扫描预计算排序，适用于极高维场景，兼具可解释性和计算可行性，解决了特征排序中截断阶段缺乏理论指导的难题。

Abstract: Feature rankings are widely used in supervised feature selection because they are simple, scalable and easy to interpret. Variables are first ranked by a relevance score, and a subset is then obtained by retaining the top-ranked variables. Although the first stage has been extensively studied, the second is often governed by an arbitrary cardinality, an empirical threshold or cross-validation, without a direct interpretation. This raises a basic question: given a feature ranking, when is there enough accumulated class-separation evidence to stop selecting features?
  This paper develops a distributional framework for transforming supervised feature rankings into class-independent subsets through an explicit risk-calibrated stopping rule. For each variable and each pair of classes, marginal separation is measured by the Bhattacharyya coefficient between the corresponding class-conditional distributions. The proposed method selects a single global subset shared by all classes by retaining the shortest prefix of a ranking whose residual product overlap falls below a prescribed threshold for every relevant class contrast. We derive binary and multiclass Bayes-risk bounds for the labelled product marginal problem, and obtain prior-dependent and prior-free calibrations of the residual-overlap threshold from a target all-pairs risk level.
  An empirical comparison on high-dimensional genomic datasets illustrates that the rule can reduce tens of thousands of variables to a few dozen while maintaining predictive performance statistically comparable to the all-features baseline. As the stopping rule only requires one-dimensional marginal overlap estimates and scans a precomputed ranking, it is well suited to very high-dimensional settings where exhaustive subset search is infeasible and interpretable truncation of feature rankings is essential.

</details>


### [122] [Diffusing Blame: Task-Dependent Credit Assignment in Biologically Plausible Dual-Stream Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31700)
*Yutaro Yamada,Luca Grillotti,Rujikorn Charakorn,Sebastian Risi,David Ha,Robert Tjarko Lange*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模误差路由（Modulo Error Routing）扩展误差扩散（Error Diffusion）算法，在严格遵循Dale原则的双流兴奋/抑制架构中，实现了MNIST 96.7%和CIFAR-10 61.7%的分类性能，并将该方法与PPO结合在连续控制和开放探索任务上取得了与无反向传播基线相当的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经回路遵循Dale原则（每个神经元突触统一为兴奋性或抑制性），而人工神经网络通常忽略这一约束。现有生物可塑性学习规则虽避免了权重转置运输问题，但在Dale原则下难以超越MNIST的简单任务。本文旨在探索误差扩散算法在严格Dale原则下是否能扩展到监督分类和强化学习的大规模任务。

Method: 提出模误差路由（Modulo Error Routing）将误差扩散从二分类扩展到多分类；引入三项领域特定创新：层特定sigmoid宽度、批次中心类误差信号、非对称初始化；在强化学习中，将ED与PPO集成（ED-PPO）。

Result: MNIST达96.7%，CIFAR-10达61.7%基线；消融实验显示MNIST和CIFAR-10上三项创新的重要性排序相反，揭示了任务依赖的信用分配瓶颈；ED-PPO在Google Brax连续控制和Craftax开放探索任务上与直接反馈对齐（DFA）基线性能相当。

Conclusion: 严格遵循Dale原则的双流架构在有监督分类和强化学习中均可实现有效的表示学习，且误差扩散方法在两种场景下均展现出与无反向传播基线竞争的能力，任务依赖的信用分配瓶颈需多基准评估才能暴露。

Abstract: Biological neural circuits obey Dale's principle: each neuron's synapses are uniformly excitatory or inhibitory. Artificial networks that respect this constraint must coordinate separate excitatory and inhibitory populations, fundamentally changing how credit is assigned during learning. Several biologically plausible learning rules avoid backpropagation's weight transport requirement, but it has been difficult to achieve strong performance under Dale's principle beyond MNIST. Error Diffusion (ED) was originally proposed in a dual-stream excitatory/inhibitory architecture, where learning is driven by routing global error signals to all layers without transporting transposed forward weights or relying on random feedback matrices. Whether such a rule can scale under Dale's principle across both supervised classification and reinforcement learning remains unknown. Here, we introduce modulo error routing to extend Error Diffusion beyond binary classification, and show that a dual-stream excitatory/inhibitory architecture trained with this method achieves 96.7% on MNIST and establishes a 61.7% baseline on CIFAR-10, demonstrating that representation learning is possible even when strictly enforcing Dale's principle. For the classification setting, we introduce three domain-specific innovations: layer-specific sigmoid widths, batch-centered class error signals, and asymmetric initialization, and ablation analysis reveals that their relative importance reverses between MNIST and CIFAR-10, exposing task-dependent credit-assignment bottlenecks invisible to single-benchmark evaluation. In reinforcement learning, we integrate ED with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and evaluate it on continuous-control tasks in Google Brax and on Craftax, an open-ended exploration task. We show that ED-PPO achieves competitive performance relative to Direct Feedback Alignment, a backpropagation-free baseline.

</details>


### [123] [Nonlinearity-Aware LoRA: Structured Gate Adaptation under Low-Rank Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31717)
*Shuai Yuan,Sudong Cai,Bingzhi Chen,Shuyuan Zheng,Chuan Xiao,Makoto Onizuka,Rui Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出LoRA在自门控Transformer前馈网络中的视图不完整，因为它忽略了低秩更新对门控选择权重的影响。作者提出NA-LoRA，通过导数时序重要性掩码和激活特定步长缩放规则，解决选择错位问题，无辅助损失和推理开销，在语言模型微调和视觉语言迁移任务上优于LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法将微调视为更新空间近似，但在自门控FFN中，低秩更新会改变非线性选择权重，导致选择错位，现有方法未考虑这一非线性特性。

Method: 提出NA-LoRA，包含两个轻量机制：1）基于导数的时序重要性掩码，用于门控相关LoRA更新；2）激活特定步长缩放规则，在有粗粒度有效同质性划分时生效。训练完成后无推理开销。

Result: 在语言模型微调和视觉语言迁移基准上，NA-LoRA一致优于原始LoRA，且与强PEFT变体相比具有竞争力或更优。

Conclusion: 非线性感知原则对于自门控FFN的参数高效微调至关重要，NA-LoRA通过轻量机制有效解决选择错位问题，验证了该原则的有效性。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is commonly viewed as an update-space approximation to full fine-tuning, yet this view is incomplete for self-gated Transformer feed-forward networks. In gated FFNs, a low-rank residual can change not only projected features but also the nonlinear selection weights that determine which channels contribute to the output. We formalize this effect as selection misalignment and connect it to the local effective homogeneity of self-gated activations. This motivates a nonlinearity-aware principle for parameter-efficient fine-tuning: low-rank updates should allocate capacity to gate channels whose nonlinear states remain responsive and should shape the temporal evolution of selection. We propose NA-LoRA, a training-only method with two lightweight mechanisms: a derivative-based temporal-importance mask for gate-related LoRA updates and an activation-specific step-scaling rule when a meaningful coarse effective-homogeneity partition is available. NA-LoRA adds no auxiliary loss and incurs no inference-time overhead. Experiments on language-model fine-tuning and vision-language transfer benchmarks show that NA-LoRA consistently improves over vanilla LoRA and is competitive with or better than strong PEFT variants.

</details>


### [124] [FedXDS: Leveraging Model Attribution Methods to counteract Data Heterogeneity in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31742)
*Maximilian Andreas Hoefler,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出 FedXDS 方法，利用 XAI 特征归因技术指导联邦学习中的选择性数据共享，以缓解客户端数据异构导致的性能下降，并结合度量隐私保护，在多个异构场景下实现了更高精度和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI 方法在模型可解释性方面取得了显著成功，但其在联邦学习等相邻机器学习领域中的潜力尚未被探索。联邦学习面临客户端数据分布统计异构导致的性能退化问题，现有方法难以精准识别哪些数据元素需要共享以缓解异构性。

Method: 提出 FedXDS，利用基于传播的特征归因技术，通过单次反向传播识别任务相关特征，实现客户端之间的选择性数据共享；同时引入度量隐私技术提供形式化隐私保证，保护敏感信息。

Result: 实验结果表明，FedXDS 在不同客户端数量和异构性设置下，一致地实现了更高的准确率和更快的收敛速度；同时提供了理论隐私保证，并在成员推断攻击和特征反转攻击下展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文首次将 XAI 引入联邦学习场景，验证了特征归因技术可有效指导数据共享以缓解统计异构问题，为 XAI 在联邦学习及其他相邻领域的应用开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods have demonstrated significant success in recent years at identifying relevant features in input data that drive deep learning model decisions, enhancing interpretability for users. However, the potential of XAI beyond providing model transparency has remained largely unexplored in adjacent machine learning domains. In this paper, we show for the first time how XAI can be utilized in the context of federated learning. Specifically, while federated learning enables collaborative model training without raw data sharing, it suffers from performance degradation when client data distributions exhibit statistical heterogeneity. We introduce FedXDS (Federated Learning via XAI-guided Data Sharing), the first approach to utilize feature attribution techniques to identify precisely which data elements should be selectively shared between clients to mitigate heterogeneity. By employing propagation-based attribution, our method identifies task-relevant features through a single backward pass, enabling selective data sharing that aligns client contributions. To protect sensitive information, we incorporate metric privacy techniques that provide formal privacy guarantees while preserving utility. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach consistently achieves higher accuracy and faster convergence compared to existing methods across varying client numbers and heterogeneity settings. We provide theoretical privacy guarantees and empirically demonstrate robustness against both membership inference and feature inversion attacks. Code is available at https://github.com/MaxH1996/FedXDS.

</details>


### [125] [Bridging the Gap Between Latent and Explicit Reasoning with Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31779)
*Ying Fan,Anej Svete,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOTUS是一种基于循环Transformer的潜在链式思维方法，通过并行监督潜在表征中的CoT步骤，首次在3B参数规模上达到与显式CoT相当的性能，同时延迟降低2.5-6.9倍，且潜在空间具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在CoT方法在超过1B参数时性能显著落后于显式CoT，且差距随规模扩大而增大。循环Transformer天然适合潜在推理，但此前未被用于弥补这一差距。

Method: 提出LOTUS：使用循环填充Transformer，并行处理K个潜在块并迭代R次，对每个潜在位置的金标准CoT步骤施加交叉熵损失（类似显式CoT监督）。

Result: 首次在3B参数规模下达到与显式CoT相当的性能；推理阶段延迟降低2.5-6.9倍；通过将循环后的潜在向量投影到头可恢复金标准推理步骤甚至发现替代有效步骤。

Conclusion: 循环Transformer配合并行潜在监督能有效弥合潜在CoT与显式CoT之间的性能差距，且潜在空间具有可解释性和CoT对齐性。循环骨干网络和并行金标准CoT监督两者缺一不可。

Abstract: Language models typically reason via explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), generating intermediate steps token-by-token. Latent CoT offers an alternative: it performs multi-step reasoning in the model's hidden states, replacing decoded tokens with continuous representations for greater efficiency. However, existing latent CoT methods underperform explicit CoT beyond 1B parameters, and the gap widens with scale. Looped, or recurrent-depth, Transformers, which reuse their weights to increase computation depth without adding parameters, are a natural fit for latent reasoning. We therefore ask whether looped Transformers can bridge this gap. We answer affirmatively with a simple recipe: a looped padded Transformer that processes K latent blocks in parallel for R iterations, with a cross-entropy loss on each latent position's gold CoT-step token, similar to explicit CoT supervision. We instantiate it as LOTUS (Looped Transformers with parallel supervision on latents). LOTUS is, to our knowledge, the first latent-CoT method to bridge the gap to explicit CoT at the 3B scale, while cutting thought-phase latency by 2.5x-6.9x from compact math expressions to natural language. Projecting LOTUS's post-loop latents through the base LM head recovers the gold reasoning steps and even surfaces alternative valid intermediate steps, evidence that its latent space is interpretable and CoT-aligned. Ablations confirm that both the looped backbone and the parallel supervision on gold CoT tokens are essential.

</details>


### [126] [Addressing Over-Refusal in LLMs with Competing Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31748)
*Taeyoun Kim,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEAR通过让模型主动进行有害推理探索（作为对抗优化中的推理方），再通过答案方生成安全回应，利用过程奖励稳定优化，从而在保持安全性的同时缓解过度拒绝问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全训练导致模型在无害提示上过度拒绝，即使使用RL推理训练也无法根本解决推理成为预判回应的"橡皮图章"问题。

Method: 将安全-拒绝权衡建模为对抗优化问题：推理方探索有害推理策略，答案方确保输出安全；使用单一模型通过过程奖励在多段思维链中扮演两个角色。

Result: SEAR模型主动进行有害推理作为探索，同时可靠地翻转回安全答案；缓解了过度拒绝问题，并能防御直接操控推理生成有害内容的攻击。

Conclusion: 有害推理可以作为一种有用的探索信号，通过解决歧义来提升模型区分有害/无害提示的能力，实现安全性与合规性的更好平衡。

Abstract: Safety training on language models often induces over-refusal: improved safety on harmful prompts at the cost of increased refusal on harmless ones. Though this trade-off can be mitigated by training models with reinforcement learning (RL) to reason before answering, it does not remove the underlying problem that reasoning can often be a "rubber stamp" for a predetermined response. In this paper, we address the safety-refusal trade-off by rethinking how models are trained to reason about safety. Our key insight is that unsafe reasoning can itself serve as a useful exploratory signal. Rather than preemptively blocking harmful thoughts, we encourage the model to sufficiently explore unsafe reasoning but produce a safe response. The harmful exploration improves the model's ability to distinguish harmful from harmless prompts by resolving ambiguity, allowing it to remain safe while complying only when appropriate. We cast this as an adversarial optimization problem in which a reasoning player explores strategies for producing an unsafe response and an answer player ensures that the final output is safe. We train a single model with dense rewards to play both roles within one chain-of-thought, across different segments. To achieve this, we find that process rewards are crucial for stable optimization of competing objectives. Our resulting model SEAR deliberately engages in harmful reasoning as exploration while reliably flipping back to a safe answer. We demonstrate that this behavior helps mitigate over-refusal and defend against attacks that directly manipulate the reasoning to be harmful.

</details>


### [127] [Review Residuals: Update-Conditioned Residual Gating for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31859)
*Kyle Kramer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Review Residuals，通过学习输入依赖的门控来缩放残差更新，该门控同时基于当前状态和提议的更新。实验表明该方法在深层网络中训练稳定，且在大规模模型（590M-1B参数）上显著优于标准残差连接和Highway门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准残差连接以固定系数1添加每个子层的更新，网络在提交更新前从不评估其可靠性。受人为因素中独立验证原则的启发，希望引入一种机制来评估更新是否可靠。

Method: 提出Review Residuals，更新公式为 h_l = h_{l-1} + r_l * u_l，其中门控 r_l = sigmoid(W[RMSNorm(h_{l-1}), RMSNorm(u_l)])。门控同时基于当前状态和提议的更新进行条件化，这是与之前门控和缩放残差的关键区别。

Result: 两个关键发现：1) 深度稳定性：凸形式（Highway风格）的门控在超过20层时重新引入梯度消失问题，而加性的、保恒等映射的形式在所有测试深度下稳定训练。2) 随规模涌现：在60M-1B参数的五种规模上训练，Review Residuals在小规模下无优势，但在590M参数时显著优于参数匹配的Highway门控和标准残差（p<0.05），在1B时优势更大。优势随模型规模增长而非缩小。

Conclusion: Review Residuals是一种有效的残差连接改进方法，在大规模模型中展现出显著优势，且其优势随模型规模扩大而增加，具有较好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Residual connections add every sublayer's proposed update with a fixed coefficient of one; the network never evaluates whether an update is reliable before committing it. Drawing on the human-factors principle of independent verification, we introduce Review Residuals, which scale each update by a learned, input-dependent gate conditioned on both the current state and the proposed update: h_l = h_{l-1} + r_l * u_l with r_l = sigmoid(W[RMSNorm(h_{l-1}), RMSNorm(u_l)]). Conditioning the gate on the update is the property that distinguishes it from prior gated and scaled residuals. We report two findings. First, a depth-stability result: a convex (Highway-style) form of the gate reintroduces vanishing gradients and fails to train beyond ~20 layers, whereas the additive, identity-preserving form trains stably at all depths we tested. Second, an emergence-with-scale result: trained from scratch across five sizes (60M-1B parameters, multi-seed), Review Residuals show no advantage at small scale but at 590M significantly outperform both a parameter-matched Highway gate and a parameter-matched standard residual (p<0.05), with a larger advantage at 1B. The benefit grows with model size rather than shrinking.

</details>


### [128] [Policy Optimization Achieves Data-Dependent Regret Bounds in MDPs with Unknown Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31769)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对转移核未知的在线情节式表格型马尔可夫决策过程，提出一种基于乐观跟随正则化领导者的策略优化算法，实现了在未知转移下的数据依赖遗憾界（一阶、二阶、路径长度）以及随机环境下的间隙依赖 poly-log 遗憾，关键创新在于乐观 Q 函数估计器与数据依赖转移奖励的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作在转移核已知时能通过策略优化同时适应对抗性与随机性损失并获得数据依赖遗憾界，但在转移核未知时是否仍能实现类似界尚待解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于乐观跟随正则化领导者（OFTRL）框架，设计了新的乐观 Q 函数估计器，并引入数据依赖的转移奖励（transition bonus），通过损失预测误差控制估计偏差。算法在更新策略时同时考虑对转移核的估计误差。

Result: 在未知转移核下，获得了与已知转移核相当的一阶、二阶和路径长度遗憾界，同时新增了一个转移依赖的复杂度项（捕获估计转移核的内在代价）。在随机环境下实现了间隙依赖的 poly-log(T) 遗憾。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明策略优化在未知转移下也能达到最优的数据依赖遗憾界，识别出转移估计带来的固有复杂度项，为在线强化学习中的自适应策略优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for online episodic tabular Markov decision processes with unknown transition kernels, aiming for best-of-both-worlds guarantees together with data-dependent regret bounds. Recent work (Dann et al., 2023; Li et al., 2026) has shown that policy optimization can adapt to both adversarial and stochastic losses with first-order, second-order, and path-length bounds, but only under known transitions, leaving open whether such data-dependent guarantees are achievable by policy optimization when the transition kernel is unknown. We resolve this by developing a new algorithm based on optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader that attains these guarantees under unknown transitions. The key ingredient is a new design of optimistic $Q$-function estimators together with a data-dependent transition bonus that controls estimator bias through the loss-prediction error. Our analysis further identifies an unavoidable transition-dependent complexity term that captures the intrinsic cost of estimating the transition kernel. As a result, we obtain first-order, second-order, and path-length bounds with the transition-dependent complexity term while simultaneously achieving gap-dependent $\mathrm{polylog}(T)$ regret in the stochastic regime.

</details>


### [129] [Signed-Permutation Coordinate Transport for RMSNorm Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31963)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明LLM残差流存在架构依赖的规范群：LayerNorm为置换群S_d，RMSNorm为符号置换群B_d。符号边缘化匈牙利匹配消除了符号相关性匹配的性能上限。基于坐标保持的规范传输在跨运行坐标恢复、SAE重建、情感操控、拒绝操控、合并对齐等任务上显著优于纯置换对齐。规范扫描审计表明索引级可解释性声明仅相对于显式规范成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工作流在不同检查点间传输坐标索引对象（如引导向量、稀疏自编码器、top-k神经元集等）时，未意识到残差流存在架构依赖的规范（gauge）自由度。作者发现LayerNorm和RMSNorm具有不同的规范群结构，而当前普遍使用的纯置换对齐对于RMSNorm模型是规范不完全的，导致工具传输失败。

Method: 1) 理论推导：LayerNorm残差图具有置换规范S_d（全局符号翻转下同），RMSNorm具有符号置换规范B_d = S_d ⋉ {±1}^d。2) 提出符号边缘化匈牙利匹配算法。3) 设计坐标保持的规范传输框架，沿同基微调轨迹组合本地B_d规范。4) 进行跨运行坐标恢复、SAE重建、情感/拒绝操控、合并对齐等实验验证。

Result: 跨运行坐标恢复：1500步坐标保持传输恢复91.1% vs 端点匹配60.3%；TinyLlama SAE重建NMSE：B_d下0.004 vs S_d下1.08；Qwen情感操控效果保持95.8% vs 17.2%；拒绝操控在S_d下符号反转；AdamW状态传输：坐标保持恢复原始轨迹，纯置换偏离。规范扫描审计表明可解释性声明仅相对于显式规范成立。

Conclusion: LLM残差流坐标索引对象的跨检查点传输必须考虑架构依赖的规范结构。RMSNorm模型必须使用符号置换规范B_d而非纯置换S_d。坐标保持的规范传输比函数级合并更基础，且同一协方差控制着有状态训练。索引级可解释性声明应始终附带显式规范说明。

Abstract: Modern LLM workflows move coordinate-indexed objects across checkpoints: steering vectors, sparse autoencoders, top-$k$ neuron sets, attribution lists, and merge alignments. This is only well posed after fixing the model's residual-stream gauge, which we show is architecture-dependent: LayerNorm residual charts have permutation gauge $S_d$ (up to a global sign flip), while RMSNorm charts with generic per-channel gain have signed-permutation gauge $B_d = S_d \ltimes \{\pm 1\}^d$. Permutation-only alignment is therefore symmetry-incomplete for RMSNorm models. We introduce sign-marginalized Hungarian matching and prove a sharp failure mode: with decorrelated coordinates, raw signed-correlation matching has a structural permutation-accuracy ceiling at the positive-sign fraction of the true gauge, which sign-marginalization removes. We then make coordinate-preserving transport, not function-level merging, the primary object: composing saved-checkpoint local $B_d$ gauges along same-base fine-tuning trajectories recovers 91.1% of cross-run coordinates at 1500 steps versus 60.3% for endpoint matching, and the gain is not explained by merely routing through the base. The recovered gauge transfers tools that permutation-only alignment breaks: TinyLlama SAE reconstruction has NMSE 0.004 under $B_d$ versus 1.08 under $S_d$; Qwen sentiment steering preserves 95.8% of its effect versus 17.2%; refusal steering reverses sign under $S_d$; coordinate-preserving merges behave the same way. The same covariance governs stateful training: signed transport of AdamW state preserves the resumed trajectory, while permutation-only state follows a different one from a functionally identical checkpoint. Finally, gauge-sweep audits show index-level interpretability claims are reproducible only relative to an explicit gauge.

</details>


### [130] [SemRF: A Semantic Reference Frame for Residual-Stream Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32022)
*Jian Gu,Aldeida Aleti,Chunyang Chen,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义参考系（SemRF）框架，将语义测量与残差动力学分离，通过锚点固定、伪逆绑定和Voronoi追踪，将语言模型的深度计算建模为语义轨迹，并建立与参数效率的条件性联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差流分析中，嵌入锚点与解嵌读出点对选定跨度存在分歧，导致观测到的表示运动可能反映的是测量漂移而非真实计算过程。需要一种形式化方法将语义测量与残差动力学分离开来。

Method: 提出SemRF框架：1) 固定锚点并测量状态相对于锚点的语义坐标；2) 通过伪逆绑定实现精确同步，在受限双可逆条件下给出稳定语义基坐标和失真界；3) 锚点诱导语义Voronoi图，距离（如logits）将每层分配到粗粒度单元；4) 定义margin松弛管道内的最小作用量路径作为规范迹，需满足离散样条方程，且当二次权为正时唯一；5) 通过参数到轨迹的映射，建立与参数效率的条件性联系。

Result: 低曲率意味着分段线性可压缩性和局部知识密度；更低迹复杂度意味着更少的语义节点；在拟合数据的可容许设定中，更低作用量和更低复杂度的迹使用更少的语义自由度。保证需要受控的接口误差和显式管道约束下的小投影残差。

Conclusion: SemRF提供了一个严谨的形式化框架，用于分离语义测量与残差动力学，通过Voronoi追踪和最小作用量原理刻画语言模型深度计算的结构特性，并建立了迹复杂度与参数效率之间的条件性联系。

Abstract: Residual-stream analysis asks how language-model computation evolves across depth, but intermediate decoding requires comparable readout coordinates across layers. If embedding anchors and unembedding readout disagree on the chosen span, apparent motion may reflect measurement drift rather than computation. We introduce \emph{Semantic Reference Frames} (SemRF), an anchor-based formalism separating semantic measurement from residual dynamics. A SemRF fixes anchors and measures states against them. Pseudo-inverse tying gives exact synchronization; under restricted bi-invertibility, SemRF yields stable semantic-basis coordinates, distortion bounds, and near-identity changes. With the frame fixed, residual computation becomes a depthwise semantic trajectory. The anchors induce a semantic Voronoi diagram: distance, or evidence such as logits, assigns each layer to a coarse cell, while coordinates retain within-cell motion and margins. We define layerwise steps, contribution profiles, and imbalance diagnostics, then use the Voronoi trace to define a margin-relaxed tube. The canonical trace is the minimum-action path inside this tube; when nonempty with positive quadratic weight, it is unique and obeys a discrete spline equation away from active constraints. Excess action controls step, curvature, and profile mismatch. Low curvature implies piecewise-linear compressibility and local knowledge density: lower trace complexity means fewer semantic knots. Through the parameter-to-trajectory map, this gives a conditional link to parameter efficiency: among admissible settings fitting data, lower-action and lower-complexity traces use fewer semantic degrees of freedom. The guarantees require controlled interface error and small projection residual under explicit tube constraints.

</details>


### [131] [Relational and Sequential Conformal Inference for Energy Time Series over Graphs via Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31804)
*Keivan Faghih Niresi,Alice Cicirello,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STOIC框架，将空间-时间图神经网络与表格基础模型的零样本上下文学习相结合，用于能源系统中的不确定性量化，在多个基准上优于现有符合预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能源需求点预测方法（如STGNN）虽表现优异，但无法提供可靠的不确定性估计；现有的符合预测方法未能充分捕捉能源系统的复杂时空结构。

Method: STOIC框架：1) 使用STGNN生成点预测；2) 将时空残差重构为表格表示；3) 利用表格基础模型通过上下文学习校准预测区间，无需特定任务重训练。

Result: 在五个基准（含合成模拟、真实电力和区域供热网络）上，STOIC持续优于现有符合预测基线，提供更可靠鲁棒的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: STOIC成功将图结构预测与表格基础模型的零样本校准能力结合，为复杂图结构能源时间序列提供了更优的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Accurate energy demand forecasting is essential for the reliable operation and planning of modern sustainable energy systems. Spatial-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) have recently achieved strong performance in point forecasting by jointly modeling temporal dynamics and relational dependencies across interconnected energy nodes. However, in real-world energy systems, accurate point forecasts alone are insufficient, as operators also require reliable uncertainty estimates to support risk-aware decision-making, grid stability, and operational planning under uncertainty. Conformal prediction provides a principled and model-agnostic framework for uncertainty quantification with statistical coverage guarantees, making it particularly attractive for safety-critical energy applications. However, existing conformal prediction approaches often fail to fully capture the complex spatial-temporal structure of energy systems. To address these limitations, we propose STOIC (Spatial-Temporal Graph Conformal Prediction with In-Context Learning), a novel framework that integrates graph-based forecasting with the zero-shot calibration capabilities of tabular foundation models. STOIC first generates point forecasts using an STGNN and subsequently reformulates spatial-temporal residuals into a tabular representation suitable for in-context learning. Leveraging a tabular foundation model, STOIC calibrates prediction intervals without task-specific retraining, effectively capturing both sequential and relational dependencies. We evaluate STOIC on five diverse benchmarks, including synthetic simulations as well as real-world electricity and district heating networks. Across all datasets, STOIC consistently outperforms existing conformal prediction baselines, delivering more reliable and robust uncertainty estimates for complex graph-structured energy time series.

</details>


### [132] [QVal: Cheaply Evaluating Dense Supervision Signals for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32034)
*Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez,Matteo Merler,Ilze Amanda Auzina,Joschka Strüber,Ameya Prabhu,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QVal，一个无需训练的测试平台，通过Q值对齐度直接评估密集监督信号的质量。在21种方法、4种环境、6种模型上的实验表明，简单提示基线优于近期复杂方法，且性能按方法族聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集监督方法在下游任务中评估，成本高昂且引入训练工程混杂因素，不同方法族因训练配置差异难以公平比较，缺乏统一基准。

Method: 提出QVal测试平台，通过计算密集监督信号与强参考策略的Q值之间的对齐程度（Q-aligned）来评估信号质量，无需模型训练即可直接比较不同方法。

Result: 在QVal-v1.0上对21种密集监督方法、4种环境、7个方法族、6种开源模型进行1200+实验，发现简单提示基线优于近期复杂方法，性能按方法族强烈聚类。

Conclusion: QVal为密集监督方法提供了可扩展的公平比较基准，使研究者能在训练前迭代改进信号质量，分离信号质量与工程实现的影响。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act over long horizons, where a single trajectory can contain hundreds or thousands of actions. In these settings, outcome-only rewards provide too sparse guidance, failing to inform the model about the goodness of intermediate actions. Dense supervision methods aim to solve this problem by scoring intermediate steps, from intrinsic confidence to self-distillation and embedding similarities. However, it is common practice to evaluate them by measuring the downstream performance of a training pipeline that integrates them. This is expensive, conflates supervision quality with training engineering confounders, and renders different methodological families requiring distinct training setups incomparable. As a result, dense supervision methods are rarely benchmarked on common ground. We introduce QVal, a training-free testbed for directly evaluating dense supervision signals. Given a state-action pair, QVal measures how well a method's score is Q-aligned: whether it orders actions according to the Q-values of a strong reference-policy. This lets us compare signals before any training run and separate signal quality from other engineering choices. We instantiate QVal as QVal-v1.0, benchmarking 21 dense supervision methods across four diverse environments and seven methodological families, with over 1.2K evaluation experiments across six open-weight model backbones. We find that simple prompting baselines consistently outperform recent dense supervision methods from the literature, and that performance clusters strongly by family. These findings hold across model sizes, environments, and observation modalities. QVal is designed to be easily extensible to new environments and methods, enabling researchers to iterate on dense supervision methods before any training run.

</details>


### [133] [Geometry-Preserving Orthonormal Initialization for Low-Rank Adaptation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31813)
*Ruijia Zhang,Jiacheng Zhu,Hanqing Zhu,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LoRA及其变体在基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中的初始化问题，提出几何保持的正交初始化方法（RLPO和RLMO），在数学推理基准上取得更好效果，并理论解释了PiSSA和MiLoRA在RLVR下表现不佳的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA及其变体在监督微调（SFT）中表现良好，但在基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）下的效果和训练稳定性尚未被充分理解。特别是PiSSA和MiLoRA在RLVR中可能不如标准LoRA甚至出现训练不稳定，这促使作者探索RLVR下低秩矩阵的初始化方法。

Method: 首先从理论上分析了LoRA在RLVR中的表现，证明正交初始化能够最小化LoRA输出与全参数微调输出之间的差距。基于这一理论洞见，提出几何保持的正交初始化方法，衍生出两个新变体RLPO和RLMO。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，提出的正交初始化方法能够稳定RLVR训练，且性能优于标准LoRA，这与PiSSA和MiLoRA的表现形成对比。

Conclusion: 正交初始化在RLVR中优于其他初始化策略；统一的理论分析也解释了PiSSA和MiLoRA在RLVR下表现不佳的原因，具有独立参考价值。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and its variants enable parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models under the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) paradigm. However, their efficacy and behavior under Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) are less well understood. In particular, two structurally initialized LoRA variants, PiSSA and MiLoRA, which outperform standard LoRA under SFT, can underperform standard LoRA under RLVR and may even exhibit training instability. These observations suggest that how to initialize the low-rank matrices in RLVR remains unclear. In this work, we develop a theoretical analysis of LoRA in RLVR, showing that orthonormal initialization achieves the minimal gap between LoRA outcome and that of full fine-tuning. Guided by this insight, we propose geometry-preserving orthonormal initialization for low-rank adaptation in RLVR, leading to two new variants, RLPO and RLMO. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed orthonormal initialization stabilizes RLVR training and outperforms standard LoRA, contrasting with PiSSA and MiLoRA. Finally, our unified analysis for LoRA initialization also explains why PiSSA and MiLoRA can underperform in RLVR, which may be of independent interest. Code and checkpoints are publicly available at https://github.com/Richard-ZZZ/geometry-preserving-orthonormal-init-rlvr.

</details>


### [134] [Low-dimensional topology of deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31856)
*Junyu Ren,Lek-Heng Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将每层宽度限制为d=3（ℝ³表示空间），研究前馈网络、ResNet和Transformer等分层模型如何通过各层改变低维拓扑不变量（主要关注环绕数）。研究发现：在改变环绕数的能力上，ResNet的跳跃连接与Transformer的注意力机制同等强大；ResNet和Transformer严格强于带单调激活的前馈网络，后者又强于可逆流模型；但将单调激活替换为非单调激活可使前馈网络达到与ResNet和Transformer同等的表达能力。结果提示低维拓扑可作为AI架构设计的指导工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过将网络每层宽度限制在d=3（ℝ³），追踪神经网络各层如何改变低维拓扑不变量，从而隔离激活函数和深度的影响，避免宽度增加对拓扑结构的简化效应（如任何纽结在ℝ⁴中都可解结），并利用ℝ³的可视化优势来深入理解不同架构的表达能力差异。

Method: 将每层宽度限制为d=3（即ℝ³作为表示空间），以环绕数（linking number）为主要拓扑不变量，从理论和实验两方面比较前馈网络、ResNet、Transformer和可逆流模型在改变拓扑结构方面的能力差异。分析了跳跃连接、注意力机制和激活函数单调性对拓扑表达能力的影向。

Result: (1) ResNet的跳跃连接与Transformer的注意力机制在改变环绕数方面能力相当；(2) ResNet和Transformer严格强于带单调激活的前馈网络，后者又强于可逆流模型；(3) 将单调激活替换为非单调激活可使前馈网络提升到与ResNet和Transformer同等的表达能力。

Conclusion: 低维拓扑（尤其是环绕数等不变量）可作为分析神经网络架构表达能力的有效工具，为AI架构设计提供理论指导。结果还从d=3推广到了任意d>3的维度。

Abstract: We study layered models, including feedforward networks, ResNets, and transformers, by limiting each layer to a width of $d = 3$, i.e., $\mathbb{R}^3$ as representation space. This allows us to track how a neural network changes low-dimensional topological invariants through its layers. Just about any topological structure may be simplified or even trivialized by simply increasing dimension; e.g., any knot is equivalent to an unknot in $\mathbb{R}^4$. By restricting to $\mathbb{R}^3$, we not only isolate the effects of activation and depth from that of width, we work in a space that lends itself to easy visualization. We focus on linking number here, deferring other invariants like link groups, Milnor's $\barμ$-invariants, knot types, ambient cobordisms, to a sequel. We provide full proofs and empirical experiments to justify the following insights: When measured by their power to effect changes in linking numbers, the layer-skipping feature in ResNets is as powerful as the attention mechanism in transformers; both ResNets and transformers are strictly more powerful than feedforward neural networks with monotonic activations, which are in turn more powerful than invertible and flow-based models; but replacing monotonic activation with a nonmonotonic one elevates a feedforward network into the same expressivity class as ResNets and transformers. These results suggest that low-dimensional topology can be a useful tool to guide designs of AI architectures. We also generalize our results from $d = 3$ to arbitrary $d > 3$.

</details>


### [135] [Sequential RC-TGAN: Generating Relational Time Series with Spectral Envelope Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31904)
*Mohamed Gueye,Yazid Attabi,Manuel Morales,Maxime Dumas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Seq. RC-TGAN，一种基于谱包络理论的新型损失函数，用于合成关系数据库中分类与连续时间序列的周期性模式生成，并引入两种新评估指标，实验证明优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成关系数据库中的时间序列（如交易日志）面临分类时序数据（如状态码）的周期性特征（季节性和循环性）难以通过标准编码（如one-hot）捕捉的问题，现有方法在频域保真度上存在不足。

Method: 扩展RC-TGAN为时序版本Seq. RC-TGAN，引入基于谱包络理论的可微损失函数，通过反向传播直接优化潜在周期结构；对连续时间序列采用变分高斯混合模型（VGM）离散化策略以应用频域正则化；构建具有已知理论谱包络的模拟基准数据集，并提出谱密度散度与谱包络散度两种新评估指标。

Result: 在真实数据集和模拟基准上，Seq. RC-TGAN在复现循环模式和长期季节性方面显著优于现有最先进系统，适用于分类和连续特征。

Conclusion: 基于谱包络理论的频域正则化方法能有效提升生成时间序列的周期保真度，所提评估指标为时序生成模型提供了更严格的频域评价标准。

Abstract: The generation of synthetic relational databases often involves modeling complex temporal dynamics, such as transaction logs or event sequences. A significant challenge in this domain is the handling of categorical time series (e.g., status codes), where standard encoding methods like one-hot encoding fail to capture intrinsic frequency-domain features such as seasonality and cyclicity. In this paper, we introduce Sequential RC-TGAN (Seq. RC-TGAN), a temporal extension of the RC-TGAN framework, equipped with a novel integrated loss function based on the \textit{Spectral Envelope Theory}. This differentiable loss allows the generator to directly optimize the preservation of latent periodic structures via backpropagation. While spectral envelope theory is inherently designed for categorical sequences, we extend this frequency-domain regularization to continuous time series by employing a Variational Gaussian Mixture Model (VGM) discretization strategy. To establish a mathematically rigorous evaluation standard, we simulate categorical time series governed by a parameter $α$, with exactly known theoretical spectral envelopes. Integrating these dynamic sequences into the child tables of a relational database yields a robust ground-truth benchmark for evaluating the frequency-domain fidelity of our generative framework. Furthermore, we address the lack of robust evaluation standards for relational time series by proposing two new metrics: Spectral Density Divergence and Spectral Envelope Divergence. Experimental results on real-world datasets, as well as our simulated benchmarks, demonstrate that our end-to-end approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in reproducing cyclic patterns and long-term seasonality across both categorical and continuous features.

</details>


### [136] [Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioning for Robust Cohesive Zone Model Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31921)
*Zhangyong Liang,Huanhuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种接口感知神经网络牛顿预处理器（IA-NNP），用于解决隐式准静态有限元分析中内聚区模型（CZM）软化导致的收敛困难问题。该方法通过数据驱动的方式学习界面修正，在不改变原始牵引-分离法则的前提下，分别实现了初始猜测提升（IA-NNP-Init）和迭代级非线性右预处理（IA-NNP-NL），在多个基准测试中提高了收敛性和分支恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内聚区模型在隐式准静态有限元分析中，因软化行为引入负界面切线、解跳变及牛顿盆地失配，导致上一收敛状态作为下一增量初始猜测不佳，引发收敛停滞、错误分支收敛或反复步长削减。现有方法（粘性正则化、路径跟踪、动态松弛、手动牛顿-拉夫森修改）存在改变有效响应、增加成本或依赖人工规则等局限，亟需一种通用且保真的解决方案。

Method: 将手动NR修改重新表述为基于规则的界面提升，并泛化为学习的、状态依赖的界面修正。IA-NNP仅作用于活跃界面变量，保持原始牵引-分离法则、残差组装、切线评估、历史更新和耗散检查不变。提出了两种实现：IA-NNP-Init（学习初始猜测提升）和IA-NNP-NL（迭代级非线性右预处理）。界面图特征编码开口量、牵引力、切线、损伤/历史变量、模式混合度、残差及其邻域状态。修正量有界、带置信门控，仅通过原始CZM牛顿求解接受。根等价性质证明IA-NNP改变收敛路径但不改变离散CZM解集。

Result: 在水平、圆形、双界面和活动前沿基准测试中，相比标准NR和手动NR修改，IA-NNP在困难增量上收敛性更好、分支恢复更优、失败更少，同时保持力-位移响应不变。

Conclusion: IA-NNP作为一种数据驱动的界面感知预处理器，有效解决了CZM软化导致的收敛问题，无需修改物理模型或依赖人工规则，具有良好的通用性和保真性。

Abstract: Cohesive Zone Models (CZMs) are widely used to simulate interface fracture, delamination, adhesive failure, and fiber--matrix debonding in aerospace composite structures. In implicit quasi-static finite element analyses, cohesive softening may introduce negative interface tangents, solution jumps, and Newton-basin mismatch, so the previous converged state can become a poor initial guess for the next increment. This may lead to stagnation, wrong-branch convergence, or repeated step cuts. Existing remedies, including viscous regularization, path following, dynamic relaxation, and manual Newton--Raphson (NR) modification, either alter the effective response, increase cost, or rely on hand-crafted interface rules. This work proposes an Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioner (IA-NNP) for difficult CZM increments. IA-NNP recasts manual NR modification as rule-based interface lifting and generalizes it into a learned, state-dependent interface correction. The method acts only on active interface variables and preserves the original traction--separation law, residual assembly, tangent evaluation, history update, and dissipation checks. Two realizations are developed: IA-NNP-Init for learned initial-guess lifting and IA-NNP-NL for iteration-level nonlinear right preconditioning. Interface graph features encode opening, traction, tangent, damage/history variables, mode mixity, residuals, and neighboring states. The correction is bounded, confidence-gated, and accepted only through the original CZM Newton solve. A root-equivalence property shows that IA-NNP changes the path to convergence but not the discrete CZM solution set. Tests on horizontal, circular, two-interface, and active-front benchmarks show improved difficult-increment convergence, better branch recovery, and fewer failures than standard NR and manual NR modification, while preserving the force--displacement response.

</details>


### [137] [Making Sense of Touch from the Child's View for Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31943)
*Max Whitton,Zecheng Wang,Puchen Liu,Quang Tuan Truong,Shengao Wang,Manaswi Yadamreddy,Oktay Ozel,Visista Jayanti,Saniya Sekhon,Hanna Samuel Tadesse,Lawrence Miao,Junjie Wang,Jiasen Lu,Chen Yu,Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨触觉在婴儿视觉概念学习中的作用，通过构建婴儿触觉事件编码系统和包含26.4万个两秒片段的触觉事件数据集，预训练发展性模型来量化触觉对视觉学习的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究触觉是否以及如何影响婴儿的视觉概念学习，并量化触觉在婴儿视觉学习中的重要性。

Method: 提出一个结构化的婴儿触觉事件编码系统，构建包含26.4万个两秒触觉事件片段的触觉事件数据集，并基于该数据集预训练发展性模型。

Result: 构建了26.4万两秒触觉事件片段数据集，预训练的发展性模型为触觉对婴儿学习的影响提供了有前景的见解。

Conclusion: 触觉是婴儿视觉概念学习的重要机制，通过结构化编码和模型预训练可以量化其重要性。

Abstract: Is the sense of touch a mechanism for human babies' learning of visual concepts? If so, can we quantify its importance, and to what extent do babies rely on their sense of touch for visual learning? To approach these questions in a principled way, we propose a structured coding system for baby-centric touch events, yielding a dataset of 264k two-second clips of touch events coded according to this system. Using this dataset, we pretrain developmentally grounded models that reveal promising insights into the nature of baby learning from touch.

</details>


### [138] [Amplifying Membership Signal Through Chained Regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31991)
*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MADreMIA是一个模型无关的框架，通过迭代生成轨迹增强生成式模型的成员推断和数据集推断攻击，利用记忆样本在迭代再生中表现出的高连贯性和慢退化特性，在低假阳性率下提供更强的成员证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断和数据集推断攻击依赖单次生成，信号弱且跨模态敏感性有限；影子模型训练对大型生成模型不可行，亟需可扩展且无需影子模型的推理方法。

Method: 提出MADreMIA框架，利用链式迭代生成过程（每次输出作为下次输入），通过分析记忆样本在迭代再生中的连贯性和退化速度差异来区分成员与非成员，支持白盒、灰盒和黑盒设置。

Result: 在图像自回归模型、扩散模型和语言模型上进行了全面评估，并在音频模型上展示了初步结果，验证了该方法在低假阳性率下提供更丰富信号的有效性。

Conclusion: MADreMIA通过迭代生成轨迹有效增强了跨模态的成员推断和数据集推断能力，为大型生成模型的隐私审计和版权执法提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The tendency of large generative models to memorize training data makes sample verification critical for privacy auditing and copyright enforcement. Current membership (MIA) and dataset inference (DI) attacks often rely on one-shot generations, which yield weak signals and limited sensitivity across modalities. Inspired by Model Autophagy Disorder (MAD), we introduce MADreMIA, a model-agnostic framework that enhances white-, gray-, and black-box MIA and DI. Rather than relying on shadow model training -- often infeasible for large generative models -- our framework facilitates scalable inference by leveraging inherent signals through iterative trajectories. This process utilizes chained generations across diverse modalities, where each output serves as the subsequent input, to improve membership evidence at low FPR. We demonstrate that memorized training samples exhibit significantly higher coherence and slower degradation during iterative regeneration than non-member generations. Our results show that MADreMIA provides richer signals across diverse model families and modalities; we present comprehensive evaluations for IARs, diffusion, and language models, alongside preliminary results demonstrating its potential for audio models.

</details>


### [139] [Radial Suppression Accelerates Algorithmic Generalization: A Geometric Analysis of Delayed Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32000)
*Srijan Tiwari,Aditya Chauhan,Manjot Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络在算法性训练数据上先记忆后泛化（grokking）的原因，通过几何视角揭示径向膨胀是导致记忆-泛化延迟的关键因素，并提出一种简单的范数惩罚方法可加速泛化达6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络为何在算法任务上先记忆后泛化（grokking现象）的根本原因，试图从几何和动力学角度解释这一延迟机制。

Method: 提出径向-角度分解的激活空间动力学形式化框架，推导三个可检验命题；设计单超参数范数惩罚，将激活软约束到sqrt(d)半径的超球面上，以抑制径向膨胀。

Result: 在模算术任务上，该惩罚使MLP和Transformer的grokking加速达6倍；在10M参数nanoGPT的3位数加法任务上，训练步数减半。

Conclusion: 径向膨胀是跨熵优化下记忆-泛化延迟的主要驱动力；抑制径向膨胀可诱导各向异性数据依赖的正则化、迫使角度更新、偏向更平缓的极小值，从而加速泛化。

Abstract: Why do neural networks memorize algorithmic training data long before they generalize? We present a geometric case study demonstrating that, on tasks where generalization requires discovering structured low-dimensional circuits, the memorization-generalization delay is driven by radial inflation of hidden representations under cross-entropy optimization. We formalize a radial-angular decomposition of activation-space dynamics and derive three testable propositions: (i) that penalizing radial inflation induces anisotropic, data-dependent weight regularization; (ii) that it suppresses radial gradient energy below the isotropic random baseline, forcing predominantly angular updates; and (iii) that it biases convergence toward flatter minima. To empirically validate these propositions, we study a single-hyperparameter norm penalty that softly constrains activations to a sqrt(d)-radius hypersphere. On modular arithmetic, this penalty accelerates grokking up to 6x across MLPs and Transformers, and halves training steps for a 10M-parameter nanoGPT on 3-digit addition.

</details>


### [140] [Surrogate Fidelity: When Can Open LLMs Explain Closed Ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32008)
*Philippe Chlenski,Zachariah Carmichael,Ayush Warikoo,Chia-Tse Shao,Yingxiao Ye,Aobo Yang,Vivek Miglani,Nehal Bandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了用开源模型代理闭源模型进行机械可解释性的有效性，发现预测层面的保真度显著高估了归因保真度，即模型对答案的共识不代表对推理过程的一致，白盒信号对因果归因的预测能力弱于黑盒输入消融方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性需要完全访问模型内部结构，但大多数广泛部署的语言模型（如GPT、Gemini）的API最多只暴露输出token的对数概率，导致无法直接对其内部机制进行分析。因此需要探究：在何种条件下，对开源模型的测量结果可以推论到闭源模型。

Method: 在预测、归因和表示三个层面评估代理模型的保真度。使用对数几率作为API兼容的标量读出，采用留一法归因分析，覆盖Llama、Qwen、GPT、Gemini四个系列共11个模型，比较白盒信号（注意力模式、扰动幅度）与黑盒输入消融对因果归因的预测能力。

Result: 预测保真度显著高估归因保真度：模型对答案的共识并不能保证对推理原因的一致。存在可访问性-有效性反转：白盒信号虽跨模型高度稳定，但对因果归因的预测能力弱；而黑盒输入消融方法天然捕捉了因果归因。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性并不能自动从开源模型迁移到闭源模型，仅凭预测层面的一致性不足以支持这种迁移。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability (MI) requires full access to model internals, yet the APIs for most widely deployed language models at best expose log-probabilities over output tokens. This creates a surrogate problem: when do measurements made on open models allow us to make claims about a closed model? We evaluate surrogate fidelity at the prediction, attribution, and representation levels. For binary classification tasks, log-odds provide an API-compatible scalar readout of the model's representation space, and leave-one-out attributions provide insight into model behavior. Across eleven models spanning four families (Llama, Qwen, GPT, and Gemini), we find that prediction fidelity substantially overstates attribution fidelity: models that agree on what the answer is often disagree on why. We document an access-validity inversion: white-box signals like attention patterns and perturbation magnitudes are highly stable across models but only weakly predictive of causal attributions, which black-box input ablations capture by design. Mechanistic insight does not automatically transfer to closed targets, and prediction-level agreement is insufficient to warrant such transfer. Code and results are available at https://github.com/facebookresearch/surrogate.

</details>


### [141] [CoMet: Context and Multiplicity Decomposition for Multimodal Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32012)
*Sanghyuk Chun,William Yang,Amaya Dharmasiri,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMet是一种高效的多模态大语言模型不确定性估计方法，通过将不确定性分解为上下文特定项和多样性特定项，并训练轻量级后验不确定性模块实现无需自回归生成或重复采样的高效不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型中的不确定性来源多样（包括不同模态及其关系），且在开放设定下答案无界，传统不确定性估计方法难以有效处理这些问题。

Method: 提出CoMet方法，将不确定性分解为上下文特定项（捕捉任务或提示引起的歧义）和多样性特定项（捕捉上下文确定的多个合理答案与输入的一致性）；训练一个轻量级后验不确定性模块来估计这两个量，无需自回归答案生成或重复采样。

Result: 在多种开放多模态基准、幻觉检测和多选视觉问答基准上，CoMet在保持高效性的同时一致性地优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过分解不确定性并训练后验模块，CoMet实现了高效且有效的MLLM不确定性估计，为多模态模型的"知道不知道"问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation has been a long-standing challenge in AI models; it amounts to "knowing what you don't know," and metacognition is notoriously difficult even for humans (cf. the Dunning-Kruger effect). Although it is still far from solved even in simpler classification systems, tackling it in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is becoming increasingly important. Within MLLMs, uncertainty can stem from any of the diverse sources as well as from their relationships, and further can stem from the unbounded answers in the open-ended setting. To tackle the issues, we propose CoMet, an MLLM uncertainty estimation method by decomposing uncertainty into a context-specific term and a multiplicity-specific term. The former captures ambiguity induced by the given context (e.g., task or prompt), while the latter captures how many plausible answers determined by the context remain compatible with the given input. We train a lightweight post-hoc uncertainty module to estimate these quantities, which enables efficient uncertainty estimation without autoregressive answer generation or repeated sampling. Experiments on various open-ended multimodal benchmarks, hallucination detection, and multiple-choice visual question answering benchmarks show that CoMet consistently improves uncertainty estimation over existing baselines while remaining efficient in practice. Code is available at https://github.com/princetonvisualai/comet_uncertainty

</details>


### [142] [FedLAB: Traceable Semantic Codebooks for Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32016)
*Zekai Chen,Kairui Yang,Xuaner Chen,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedLAB提出了一个可追踪语义码本框架，用于联邦多模态图基础模型学习。它通过分层码本组织模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文，在保护隐私的同时实现知识迁移和语义可追溯，在10个基准和6个下游任务上比SOTA提升最高7.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的多模态图（含文本、图像、属性、关系拓扑）通常分布在去中心化客户端，由于隐私约束，原始内容和局部结构无法集中共享。现有方法通过参数、原型、嵌入或码本交换知识，但未能显式揭示模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文如何联合支持预测，缺乏内在语义可追溯性。

Method: 提出FedLAB框架，构建类型化分层码本（模态证据码本、节点语义码本、拓扑上下文码本）来组织多模态图知识；通过联邦语义质心预训练精炼这些可追溯单元，同时保持原始多模态内容和图结构在本地。

Result: 在10个基准和6个下游任务上，FedLAB相比SOTA基线最高提升7.53%，同时保留了原生语义追踪接口。

Conclusion: FedLAB通过可追踪语义码本框架有效解决了联邦多模态图基础学习中的隐私约束与语义可追溯性矛盾，在性能提升的同时提供了明确的语义解释能力。

Abstract: Multimodal graph foundation models aim to learn reusable knowledge from graphs enriched with text, images, attributes, and relational topology, thereby supporting diverse graph-centric and modality-centric tasks. In practice, however, such multimodal graphs are often distributed across decentralized clients, where raw contents and local structures cannot be centrally shared due to privacy constraints. This motivates federated multimodal graph foundation learning, which requires not only transferable representation learning but also intrinsic semantic traceability under strict data isolation. Existing methods usually exchange or store knowledge through parameters, prototypes, embeddings, or compact codebooks, which support optimization and transfer but do not explicitly expose how modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context jointly support predictions. To bridge this gap, we propose FedLAB, a traceable semantic codebook framework that organizes multimodal graph knowledge into typed hierarchical codebooks for modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context. FedLAB further refines these trace units through federated semantic barycenter pre-training while keeping raw multimodal contents and graph structures local. Extensive experiments on 10 benchmarks and 6 downstream tasks show that FedLAB improves over state-of-the-art baselines by up to 7.53\%, while preserving a native semantic trace interface.

</details>


### [143] [TRIAGE: Role-Typed Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32017)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Jiaxin Zhang,Xinchen Du,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAGE提出了一种角色类型的信用分配框架，通过结构化法官对动作片段进行语义角色分类（决定性进展、有用探索、无进展基础设施、倒退），并基于角色条件固定规则分配过程奖励，弥补了GRPO仅依赖最终结果进行信用分配的盲点，在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop上提升了成功率，并减少了环境交互回合数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO使用最终验证器结果作为所有动作令牌的统一优势，这种信号结构上不完整：它会惩罚失败轨迹中的有用探索，并在成功轨迹中强化冗余或倒退动作。因此需要更细粒度的信用分配机制来区分不同语义角色的动作片段。

Method: 提出TRIAGE框架，引入语义角色轴来增强结果信用分配。结构化法官将每个动作片段分类为决定性进展、有用探索、无进展基础设施或倒退四个角色，然后通过角色条件固定规则将这些标签映射为有界的分段过程奖励。该方法保持了验证器结果作为优化方向来源，同时修正了仅依赖结果信用的两个主要盲点。理论证明了角色条件信用是从角色标签可表达的最优分段级校正。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop三个基准上，TRIAGE对两个策略模型的成功率均优于GRPO，且超过了标量法官推导的过程奖励和结果监督的共享骨干价值基线。消融实验表明增益来自角色类型化而非仅增加密集奖励：成功轨迹中倒退动作的可靠检测是主要贡献因素，探索信用提供了一致的次要增益。在完成的ALFWorld和WebShop轨迹上，TRIAGE相对GRPO分别减少了10.4%和14.8%的环境交互回合数。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过角色类型信用分配有效修正了GRPO中结果信号的结构缺陷，在多个智能体强化学习任务上提升了效率和效果。理论分析表明该方法是角色标签可表达的最优分段级校正，并有助于降低策略梯度方差。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning requires assigning credit to environment-facing actions such as searches, clicks, edits, navigation commands, and object interactions. Standard GRPO uses the final verifier outcome as a uniform advantage over all action tokens. This outcome signal is useful but structurally incomplete: it punishes useful exploration in failed rollouts and reinforces redundant or regressive actions in successful rollouts. We propose TRIAGE, a role-typed credit assignment framework that adds a semantic role axis to outcome credit. A structured judge classifies each segment as decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression, and a fixed role-conditioned rule maps these labels to bounded segment-level process rewards. This keeps verifier outcomes as the source of optimization direction while correcting the two main blind spots of outcome-only credit. We further show that role-conditioned credit is the optimal segment-level correction expressible from role labels alone -- a projection of the per-segment advantage residual onto the role variable -- so that the fixed role constants reduce advantage estimation error whenever the judge is reliable, and we connect this to lower-variance policy gradients. Across ALFWorld, Search-QA, and WebShop, TRIAGE improves success rates over GRPO for two policy models and outperforms both a scalar judge-derived process reward and an outcome-supervised shared-backbone value baseline. Ablations show that the gain comes from role typing rather than merely adding dense rewards: reliable detection of regression inside successful trajectories is the dominant contributor, while exploration credit provides a consistent secondary gain; on completed ALFWorld and WebShop rollouts, TRIAGE also reduces environment-facing turns by an additional $10.4\%$ and $14.8\%$ relative to GRPO.

</details>


### [144] [AdaJEPA: An Adaptive Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32026)
*Ying Wang,Oumayma Bounou,Yann LeCun,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaJEPA，一种在模型预测控制闭环中通过测试时自适应更新潜在世界模型的方法，仅需少量梯度步即可显著提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜在世界模型在测试时冻结，当预测不准确时（尤其在测试时分布偏移下）规划会失败，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: AdaJEPA在MPC闭环中执行测试时自适应：规划并执行首个动作块，利用观测到的下一状态转移作为自监督信号更新模型，然后基于更新后的模型重新规划。

Result: 在多个目标到达任务中，每次MPC重新规划仅需一个梯度步，即可显著提升规划成功率。

Conclusion: 闭环更新能持续重新校准世界模型，无需额外专家演示即可应对测试时分布偏移。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning from high-dimensional observations by predicting future states in a compact latent space. However, these models are typically kept frozen at test time: when their predictions become inaccurate, planning can fail, especially under test-time distribution shift. To address this, we propose AdaJEPA, an adaptive latent world model that performs test-time adaptation within the closed loop of model predictive control (MPC). After training, AdaJEPA plans and executes the first action chunk, uses the observed next-state transition as a self-supervised adaptation signal, and replans with the updated model. This closed-loop update continuously recalibrates the world model without additional expert demonstrations. Across a range of goal-reaching tasks, AdaJEPA substantially improves planning success with as few as one gradient step per MPC replanning step.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [What Drives Interactive Improvement from Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30774)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jan Łojek,Mikołaj Garstecki,Szymon Pobłocki,Alicja Ziarko,Piotr Miłoś*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过学生-教师协议框架，系统研究了多轮语言智能体场景中，自然语言反馈是否真正带来超越重复尝试的改进。结果表明，自我反馈几乎没有额外增益，而外部强教师的反馈才能带来显著提升；交互提升更多取决于学生利用反馈的能力而非教师身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将多轮对话中的准确率提升归因于反馈的有效性，但这一提升可能来自重采样、格式修正或额外测试时计算等混杂因素。文章旨在分离这些效应，明确自然语言反馈是否带来真实增益。

Method: 提出受控的学生-教师协议，在Omni-MATH、Codeforces、BBEH Linguini和ARC-AGI1四个基准上，评估13个开源模型作为学生和教师的表现，比较外部反馈、自我反馈和无指导自我优化，控制交互历史、任务难度及教师对特权信息的访问。

Result: 多轮提升往往不是反馈使用的证据；自我反馈几乎不带来额外增益；强外部教师能产生显著更大的反馈特异性增益；密集的学生-教师交互矩阵显示，交互增益更多由学生利用反馈的能力驱动，而非教师身份。

Conclusion: 反馈型智能体应与重复尝试基线对比评估；利用反馈行动的能力（而非反馈可用性）是交互改进的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: We study when natural-language feedback produces improvement beyond the gains obtainable from repeated attempts alone. In multi-turn language agent setting, higher final accuracy can reflect useful feedback, but it can also arise from resampling, format correction, or additional test-time computation. To separate these effects, we introduce a controlled student-teacher protocol across Omni-MATH, Codeforces, BBEH Linguini, and ARC-AGI1, evaluating thirteen open-weight models in both student and teacher roles. We compare external feedback, self-feedback, and unguided self-refinement, while varying interaction history, task difficulty, and teacher access to privileged task information. Across settings, we find that multi-turn improvement is often not evidence of feedback use: self-generated feedback adds little beyond unguided self-refinement, whereas the strongest external teachers produce substantially larger feedback-specific gains, suggesting that useful feedback must provide guidance beyond generic retry. Dense student-teacher interaction matrices further show that interactive gains are driven more by the student's ability to use feedback than by the teacher's identity, although teacher choice remains important for a fixed student. These results suggest that feedback-based agents should be evaluated against repeated-attempt baselines, and that ability to act on feedback, not merely feedback availability, is a central bottleneck for interactive improvement. We release our controlled student-teacher evaluation framework at https://j-lojek.github.io/feedback-generation-is-a-bottleneck/.

</details>


### [146] [Contrastive Reflection for Iterative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30840)
*Derek Koh,Jinghui Mo,Benjamin H. Le,Jiening Zhan,Baofen Zheng,Kevin Bevis,Nathaniel C. Owen,Lauren Elizabeth Charney,Wenqiong Liu,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Contrastive Reflection框架，一种基于对比学习的迭代提示优化方法，用于代理式信息检索工作流。通过识别错误驱动的行为切片，对比分析成功与失败案例，并利用Teacher LLM生成针对性提示修改，在HotpotQA数据集上将精确匹配准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%，优于仅失败样本或随机证据的变体，达到接近现代提示优化器MIPROv2（59.4%）和GEPA（57.0%）的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代理式IR系统中，提示词优化本质上是调试问题而非盲目搜索。工程师需要知道哪个行为失败、哪个邻近行为仍有效、两者的区别是什么，以及提示编辑是否能在不引入回归的情况下提升保留集质量。现有方法缺乏这种结构化的、可检查的对比分析机制。

Method: 框架从任务中心的质量定义出发：QA代理暴露检索或推理轨迹，评分代理暴露维度级分数和理由。利用这些结构化轨迹识别错误锚定的行为切片，添加邻近的成功示例，请求Teacher LLM提出针对性提示编辑。候选编辑仅在验证性能提升时被接受，可选回归检查。实例化采用基于树的切片选择器，但核心贡献在于对比反思循环本身。

Result: 在HotpotQA检索增强QA设置上，一次树选择的对比修复将保留集精确匹配准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%。仅失败样本和随机证据变体改进较少且破坏更多先前正确示例。指令仅比较显示方法接近MIPROv2（59.4%）和GEPA（57.0%）。

Conclusion: Contrastive Reflection是一个可解释的优化循环，旨在使IR代理的提示修复更具可检查性和验证驱动性。该方法通过结构化对比分析实现了显著的性能提升，同时保持了对回归问题的敏感性。

Abstract: LLM agents are becoming central to information retrieval: they issue retrieval queries, synthesize answers, and increasingly serve as judges for IR evaluation. Improving the prompts that control these agents is an optimization problem, but in applied IR settings it often looks less like blind search and more like debugging. Engineers need to know which behavior failed, which nearby behavior still worked, what distinguishes the two, and whether a prompt edit improves held-out quality without introducing regressions.
  We present Contrastive Reflection, an iterative prompt-optimization framework for agentic IR workflows. The framework starts from a task-centric quality definition: QA agents expose retrieval or reasoning traces, and grading agents expose dimension-level scores and rationales. These structured traces are used to identify error-anchored behavioral slices, add nearby successful examples from the same region, and ask a Teacher LLM to propose a targeted prompt edit. Candidate edits are accepted only when validation performance improves, optionally subject to regression checks. We instantiate the framework with a tree-based slice selector, but the contribution is the contrastive reflection loop rather than the tree itself.
  On a public HotpotQA retrieval-augmented QA setup, one tree-selected contrastive repair improves held-out exact-match accuracy from 51.4% to 60.4%.
